Grounded in the code against the increment's **exact new shape**: (a) the join (`milestone::join` → `JoinOutcome { overlay: BTreeMap<address, MergedDoc>, findings }`) is **already suffix-resolved** but `MergedDoc { provenance, source_task, edges }` carries **no body bytes** — `suffix_resolve` computes the rewritten body in-memory and **discards it**, keeping only the re-derived edges — so "materialize the suffix-rewritten doc bodies into the parent area" is **genuinely net-new bytes**, not a reuse of an existing materialized form (verified by reading `milestone.rs::suffix_resolve`); (b) the single-task finalize planner `engine::finalize::plan_finalize` walks `<task_dir>/docs/*.md` and promotes each by its `location:`, but **renders the message from a staged `commit:<slug>.md`** a milestone has none of — so the synthesized message is a **distinct render path**, not the commit-doc path (verified by reading `finalize.rs` phase 3 + the build-halt entry above); (c) a same-doc clash is a **blocking finding inside an `Ok(JoinOutcome)`** (the merge ran, then routed the contention — `dispatch_join`'s pattern), so milestone finalize must block on `outcome.findings.has_blocking` *before* materializing/committing. Serial spine: the deterministic synthesized message (a pure engine projection, independent of the bodies) first, then the materialize step that re-derives the suffix-rewritten bytes into a parent staging area, then the milestone-finalize transaction + `jigc milestone finalize` verb that blocks-on-clash and drives one commit through the binary, then the genuine flow-9 permutation acceptance. *Why this order:* T1 is a pure function of the milestone id + id-ordered list (no bodies, no overlay) — provable in isolation; T2 (materialize) consumes the join overlay T1 does not need; T3 wires T1's message + T2's materialized bodies into the commit boundary and is the first task to touch git; T4's permutation acceptance must run over the *whole* materialize→synthesize→commit path T1–T3 establish, so it lands last.

- **T1 — the CLI-synthesized deterministic commit message (a structural projection of the milestone id + its id-ordered sub-task list).** An engine helper `milestone::synthesized_message(milestone_id, &TaskList) -> String` (or equivalent pure fn) renders the **one** milestone-finalize commit message from the milestone id + its **id-ordered** sub-task list (`TaskList::enumerate`, never insertion/`read_dir`/feed order) — **no authored prose, no commit doc, no slot** (structure the CLI owns, like git's auto-generated merge message; [DECISIONS.md](DECISIONS.md) 2026-06-04 — the inc-4 fork resolution; [finalize.md](design/finalize.md) → `fan-out` finalize). The exact message layout (subject + the id-ordered sub-task lines) is a doc-elaboration pin the executor makes, golden-locked. **Done:** an engine unit test asserts the synthesized message for a milestone with ≥2 sub-tasks is the expected golden string, and that feeding the **same** task set under divergent insertion orders (incl. reverse) yields the **byte-identical** message (the id-sort at the boundary, hardening #7); gate green. Files: `crates/engine/src/milestone.rs`.
- **T2 — materialize the join's suffix-rewritten doc bodies into a parent staging area's `docs/`.** A new engine entry `milestone::materialize(jigc_root, repo_root, milestone_id, schemas, committed) -> Result<MaterializeOutcome, Finding>` (result shape a doc-elaboration pin) that runs `join`, **blocks** if `outcome.findings` holds any blocking finding (same-doc clash / cross-area / isolation — surfaced, nothing materialized), else writes each overlay entry's **final body bytes** into a parent staging `docs/` area keyed by the (possibly suffixed) address: a bare-slug instance's body is its sub-area body **unchanged**; a suffixed instance's body is its sub-area body with its self-references rewritten to the suffixed id (re-applying the `write::set_field` rewrite `suffix_resolve` computes-but-discards) and **renamed** to `<type>:<suffixed-slug>.md`. The materialized `docs/` is the staging form `finalize`'s promote sweep already reads (`<area>/docs/<type>:<slug>.md`). Determinism: materialization iterates the id-sorted overlay; the written bytes are a pure function of the area set ([storage.md](design/storage.md#the-by-task-id-join-m7) → "the parent working overlay, already suffix-resolved … finalize then commits the overlay"). **Done:** an engine unit test over a forced-overlap fixture (two `created` colliding slugs, the higher-id one self-referential, plus a disjoint doc) asserts materialize writes the bare-slug body unchanged and the **`-2`-suffixed** body with its **own** self-ref rewritten to the suffixed id (a fixture referencing its own slug, so a broken self-rewrite fails), all in the parent `docs/`; a clash fixture (two `edited-from-base`) asserts materialize **blocks** with the routed `join.same-doc-clash` and writes **nothing**; gate green. Files: `crates/engine/src/milestone.rs`.
- **T3 — milestone finalize transaction + `jigc milestone finalize <milestone-id>` (clap + dispatch) — one commit through the binary.** Add a `Finalize { milestone_id }` variant to the existing `MilestoneCommand` enum + a `run_finalize`/`dispatch_finalize` arm: discover the repo root + `.jigc/` home, read the milestone's shared base pin (the stamp `index::load_committed` keys to), load the shipped schemas, call T2 `materialize` (which blocks-on-clash), then drive the commit boundary — feed the materialized parent `docs/` through the existing `engine::finalize::plan_finalize`'s **promote/stage/commit** path (reused unchanged for phases 4–6: copy each materialized doc to its `location:` canonical path, `git add --all`, `git commit -F`) but with the **T1 synthesized message** substituted for the commit-doc render (a milestone has no `commit:<slug>.md`), and a base-pin == HEAD preflight; a blocking materialize finding surfaces on stderr with its route + non-zero exit (`finding_to_err`), nothing committed; a clean run lands **one** commit and prints a summary. The squash/per-sub-task-commit knob stays **deferred** ([finalize.md](design/finalize.md) → `fan-out` finalize; this increment ships the single-commit form only). The executor pins how much of `plan_finalize` is reused vs a sibling milestone-finalize planner that takes a pre-rendered message + a materialized `docs/` (a doc-elaboration pin; the promote/hash logic must not be re-implemented divergently). **Done (through the real binary):** a CLI integration test in a throwaway git repo creates a milestone, populates ≥2 sub-areas as fixtures (a clean disjoint persisted doc + a `created` collision with a self-ref), runs `jigc milestone finalize <id>`, asserts exit 0, **exactly one** new commit (`git rev-list --count`), the synthesized message (`git log -1 --format=%B`), and the promoted suffix-resolved docs present at their canonical paths in the tree; a second fixture with a same-doc clash (two `edited-from-base`) asserts a **non-zero exit** + the routed `join.same-doc-clash` and **no new commit** (`git rev-parse HEAD` + `git status --porcelain` unchanged); a clap parse test covers the new verb's arg shape; gate green. Files: `crates/cli/src/cli.rs`, `crates/cli/src/milestone.rs`, `crates/cli/src/render.rs`, `crates/cli/tests/milestone.rs`.
- **T4 — the flow-9 genuine permutation acceptance through the real binary (the headline `Proves`).** A dedicated CLI e2e (`crates/cli/tests/flow9_milestone_join.rs`, the `flow8_…`/`pack_source_determinism.rs` idiom) drives the **whole** flow 9 in a throwaway repo: `jigc milestone create` → `add-task`×N **and** an `add-from-spec` path → populate the sub-areas as fixtures **forcing genuine overlap** (two same-slug `created` with one **self-referential**, a **cross-area** ref into a sibling, an `edited-from-base` **same-doc clash** isolated to its own assertion) with a **`read_dir`-order ≠ id-order** sub-area layout → run milestone `finalize` under **≥3 divergent feed orders** (id, **reverse**, seed-shuffled) on equivalent repos and assert the committed bytes (**message *and* tree**) are **byte-identical** across all three. Plus the supporting assertions per flow 9: the suffix is assigned **by task id** (lower keeps bare, higher → `-2`), the suffixed instance's **own** self-ref is rewritten, a **cross-area** ref is **blocked** with `schema-conformance.ref-resolves` (and a control showing a naïve union would resolve it clean), and a **same-doc clash** is **blocked** with `join.same-doc-clash` (no commit). Reverse order is mandatory (an id-ordered fixture hides a completion-ordered/broken merge). **Done:** the ≥3-feed-order byte-identical (message + tree) assertion passes through the real binary over the forced-overlap, `read_dir`≠id-order fixture; the suffix-by-task-id, self-ref-rewrite, cross-area-block, and same-doc-clash-block assertions each pass; gate green. Files: `crates/cli/tests/flow9_milestone_join.rs`.

No new fork surfaced — the increment is fully covered by the milestone's settled decisions (2026-06-04 M7 planning entry + the **inc-4 fork resolution** above: milestone `finalize` ships **one** commit with a **CLI-synthesized** structural-projection message — no commit doc, no authored prose, byte-identical across feed orders; the per-sub-task variant + `finalize.fan-out.squash` knob deferred to M8+; and the confirmed-in-spec materialization of the join's suffix-rewritten **bodies** into the parent area, since the join keeps only edges today) and the locked [finalize.md](design/finalize.md) / [storage.md](design/storage.md) / [worked-examples.md](design/worked-examples.md) flow 9. **Both *Grouped scope* bullets map to a task:** bullet 1 *milestone finalize — materialize the merged overlay into the parent `docs/`, feed it into the existing finalize transaction, one commit boundary with the synthesized message* → **T2** (materialize) + **T1** (the synthesized message) + **T3** (the finalize transaction + verb through the binary); bullet 2 *the flow-9 e2e — create → add-task×N + add-from-spec → populate fixtures → join/finalize under id/reverse/shuffled → byte-identical commit; assert suffix-by-task-id, self-ref rewrite, cross-area blocked, same-doc clash blocked* → **T4** (with the through-the-binary finalize surface T3 provides). Nothing is narrowed: this is the milestone's last increment, so no scope rides to a later one; the squash/per-sub-task-commit knob is the **already-settled deferral** to M8+ ([finalize.md](design/finalize.md) → `fan-out` finalize), not a new reduction. **Verified-fact corrections to the roadmap's loose phrasing the executor must NOT trust blindly** (each a task red step): (a) the join's `MergedDoc` carries **edges, not bodies**, and `suffix_resolve` **discards** the rewritten body — so T2's materialize is **net-new byte production** (re-read the sub-area body + re-apply the self-ref rewrite), never a copy of an existing materialized overlay; (b) `plan_finalize` renders the message from a staged `commit:<slug>.md` a milestone **has none of** — so T3 must **substitute** the T1 synthesized message into the commit path, never expect the commit-doc render to produce it; (c) a same-doc clash is a **blocking finding inside `Ok(JoinOutcome)`**, so milestone finalize must block on `findings` *before* materializing/committing (the `dispatch_join` precedent). Doc-elaboration pins the executor makes (not forks): (i) the synthesized-message layout (subject + the id-ordered sub-task lines), golden-locked; (ii) the materialize result shape + the parent staging-area location (where the materialized `docs/` lives before promote — e.g. a dedicated `.jigc/milestones/<id>/merged/docs/` or the parent task area), golden-locked where bytes matter; (iii) how much of `engine::finalize::plan_finalize` T3 reuses vs a thin milestone-finalize planner taking the pre-rendered message + materialized `docs/` — the promote/stage/commit/hash logic must be **shared, not re-implemented divergently**.

T1 landed (2026-06-04): `milestone::synthesized_message(milestone_id, &TaskList) -> String` — a pure structural projection of the milestone id + its **id-ordered** sub-task list (`TaskList::enumerate`, never insertion/`read_dir`/feed order), no bodies/overlay/git, first in the serial spine. Message-layout pin (golden-locked): subject `Finalize milestone <id> (<N> sub-task[s])` (singular/plural on N), a blank line, then one `- <sub-task-id>` line per sub-task in id order, trailing newline. Byte-identical across divergent insertion orders (test feeds reverse over the same id set) — the id-sort at the boundary, hardening #7. T3 substitutes this string for the absent commit-doc render in the milestone-finalize commit path.

T2 landed (2026-06-04): `milestone::materialize(jigc_root, repo_root, milestone_id, schemas, committed) -> Result<MaterializeOutcome, Finding>` runs `join`, surfaces the **first** `Severity::Blocking` finding (same-doc clash / cross-area / isolation) before writing anything, else re-produces each id-sorted overlay entry's **final body bytes** into the parent staging `docs/`. **Pin (ii) — result shape + staging location (golden-locked where bytes matter):** the parent staging area is `<jigc_root>/milestones/<id>/merged/` (constant `MERGED_AREA = "merged"`); its `docs/<type>:<final-slug>.md` is the exact `<area>/docs/<type>:<slug>.md` staging form `finalize`'s promote sweep already reads (`instance_path` reused over the merged area). `MaterializeOutcome { docs_dir: PathBuf, addresses: Vec<String> }` carries the merged `docs/` path + the id-sorted materialized addresses (audit trail); `merged/docs/` is **truncated** before each materialize (a clean rebuild, never stale-body accretion). **Net-new byte production (verified-fact (a)):** the join's `MergedDoc` carries edges not bodies and `suffix_resolve` discards the rewritten body, so materialize re-reads the sub-area body + re-applies the rewrite via the new shared `resolved_body(milestone_id, &StagedDoc, nth, schemas) -> (final_address, body)` — a bare/lone instance's body is byte-unchanged; a suffixed instance's body has its own self-refs rewritten to the suffixed id via `write::set_field`. `suffix_resolve` now **delegates its self-ref body production to `resolved_body`** then `edges_from_source`-derives its edges from those same bytes, so the overlay edges and materialized bytes can never diverge. The fold's gather loop was extracted to `gather_groups` (shared by `fold_areas` + `materialize`, no divergent re-walk; `state::DOCS_DIR` widened to `pub(crate)`). Determinism: the overlay is id-keyed and each body is `resolved_body`'s deterministic output, so no enumeration/read_dir/completion order reaches the bytes. T3 feeds `merged/docs/` into the finalize promote/stage/commit path with the T1 synthesized message.

T3 landed (2026-06-04): `jigc milestone finalize <milestone-id>` — the milestone commit boundary, one commit through the binary. `MilestoneCommand::Finalize { milestone_id }` + its own `dispatch_finalize` arm (like `Join`, it returns an `ExitCode`, not a one-line summary). `run_milestone_finalize`: discover repo root + `.jigc/`, load shipped schemas, read the milestone's shared base pin (the stamp `load_committed` keys the committed `EdgeIndex` to), call **T2 `materialize`** (which blocks-on-clash BEFORE writing — verified-fact (c)), synthesize the message via **T1 `synthesized_message`**, then drive the commit boundary. **Pin (iii) resolved — a thin sibling planner, promote/stage/commit/hash SHARED not re-implemented:** new engine `finalize::plan_milestone_finalize(staging_dir, base, head_sha, message, has_diff, schemas) -> Result<FinalizePlan, Vec<Finding>>` reuses `plan_finalize`'s preflight (staging exists, base==HEAD → `finalize.no-task`/`finalize.base-mismatch`), empty-commit guard (`finalize.empty-commit`), and `plan_promotions` (phase 4 + phase 7 hashes) **unchanged**, but **substitutes** the caller's pre-rendered synthesized message for the absent commit-doc render (verified-fact (b)) and takes **no validate report** (the join already adjudicated). The CLI-side promote/stage/commit/rollback/post-commit was extracted from `TaskArea` methods into a free `pub(crate) execute_finalize_plan(repo_root, jigc_root, msg_tmp_dir, plan, cleanup_dir)` (+ free `promote`/`rollback_promotions`/`ensure_jigc_gitignore`/`post_commit`/`advance_file_state`), so the per-task `finalize` and the milestone path drive the **same** executor — the milestone passes its area as both `msg_tmp_dir` and `cleanup_dir` (removed on a landed commit). The staging dir fed to the planner is `materialized.docs_dir.parent()` (the `merged/` area). `has_diff` = the materialized addresses are non-empty OR `git diff <base>`/untracked non-empty (`git_head`/`git_diff`/`git_untracked` widened to `pub(crate)`). CLI integration test through the real binary: create → 2 add-tasks (non-id order) → fixtures (a disjoint `edited-from-base` ADR + a `created` collision with self-ref) → `finalize` asserts exit 0, **exactly one** new commit (`rev-list --count`), the synthesized message (`log -1 --format=%B`, id-ordered body), the three promoted docs at `decisions/<slug>.md` (incl. the `-2` instance with its self-ref rewritten in-tree + tracked), a clean working tree, and the milestone area removed; a same-doc-clash fixture (two `edited-from-base`) asserts non-zero + routed `join.same-doc-clash` + HEAD/porcelain unchanged + nothing promoted. The squash/per-sub-task-commit knob stays the already-settled M8+ deferral.

## 2026-06-04 — M7 retro: planning + harness improvements

The M7 run was the strong off-substrate result (the front-loaded bet held; hardening #7 added *before* the failure; a single Plan-halt, cleanly settled; audit e2e-pass + 2 LOW fixes). The retro folded four process gaps back into the harness — *widening disciplines, not adding checks*:

- **Integration-seam spike → [milestone-planning](implementation/milestone-planning-workflow.md) Settle.** M7's only halt (inc-4) was a *precondition mismatch* at the boring seam where new work meets proven code — "feed the merged overlay into the existing finalize transaction" assumed finalize renders a commit message, but a milestone has no commit doc. Generalized: "reuse the existing X" must verify **X's input preconditions are satisfied by the new producer**, not just that the capability exists — the reuse-is-a-claim discipline, one altitude in. Same shape as M3 (create-gate)/M4 (unwired cascade)/M5 (wrong-layer read). Adversarial energy goes to the scary novelty and skips the seam; that's where the gap hid.
- **Forward-looking harness review → a conditional Settle phase for off-substrate milestones** (was a one-time graduation pass in [ideas/self-hosting.md](ideas/self-hosting.md)). It named hardening #7 before inc-3 built — the first gate added ahead of its failure. Substrate-riding milestones skip it.
- **Milestone-keyed deferral ledger → [decisions-pending.md](implementation/decisions-pending.md) "Before planning," consulted at planning Scope.** Deferred discussions ("settle X before milestone N") were evaporating into DECISIONS prose and being forgotten; now they're keyed to a trigger milestone and resurfaced at it. Revived + generalized the stale (M1-only) `decisions-pending.md` into a two-altitude forward-look (pre-planning milestone-keyed + build-time increment-keyed) rather than spawning a second file; seeded with M8's deferrals. (The user's catch: a recurring promise-and-forget the harness had no structural guard for.)
- **[ideas/self-hosting.md](ideas/self-hosting.md) updated:** part-4 review is repeatable-per-novel-milestone; #7 is the worked *mechanizable-vs-judgment* example (obligations → probe/lint; the review that found it → agent judgment); halt→resume script-surgery and the deferral ledger logged as mechanization candidates. (Near-literal confirmation: part 4 predicted a fresh project would "re-learn a 7th failure class the hard way" — M7's review named exactly the 7th class, ahead of the build.)

## 2026-06-04 — M8 (milestone execution — control plane) planning: settled design

Planned via [`/milestone-plan`](.claude/commands/milestone-plan.md). Three `capability-auditor`s established the baseline (all eight M8 pieces genuinely net-new, nothing half-scaffolded; the design docs more settled than expected); four `gap-detector`s + the **forward-looking harness review** (the conditional Settle phase for an off-substrate milestone) shaped it. The headline baseline finding: **the M7 join's input preconditions are met only by fixture-staging today** — `copy_in` has zero production callers (so `edited-from-base`/`same-doc-clash` is unreachable from the binary), and `add-task` mints areas with no `docs/` (resume-composable, not write-ready). M8's four pre-loaded ledger items all settled (two **new** forks surfaced that the ledger didn't carry):

- **Commit shape = per-sub-task authored commit docs + the `finalize.fan-out.squash` knob** (default `true` → one CLI-synthesized aggregate commit; `false` → one commit per sub-task in id order rendering each authored commit doc, plus the parent's). *Why:* the human chose the fuller M8 over the minimal one-synthesized-commit form — it exercises finalize.md's "eventual" design end-to-end. Un-defers the knob + per-sub-task docs from M7's M8+ pin ([finalize.md](design/finalize.md#fan-out-finalize)); requires `provision_commit_doc` to record `created` provenance.
- **[NEW fork, not pre-loaded] A finalize-less sub-workflow.** `single-task`'s last step runs `jigc task finalize` (git) — but sub-agents never run git (the parent finalize is the boundary). So the fan-out's `run:` targets a new **`sub-task`** workflow (locate → implement → author-commit, no finalize), keeping `allows-create: [adr]` so real sub-agents create ADRs that genuinely exercise the join's collision-suffix/self-ref-rewrite. *Why:* a sub-agent handed unmodified `single-task` would be told to do the one thing it must never do.
- **Re-entry contract = add `jigc workflow <W> --task <id>`** (the design's named verb; `start --task` stays for top-level resume). `<W>` is explicit (the launch template fills `{{workflow}}`) and asserted-equal to the sub-task's recorded mint workflow; re-entry **provisions** the write-ready area on first entry. *Why:* the L1 landmine — the verb is named across three design docs, specified in none, and `write-commands.md` (its home) had zero coverage; now spec'd there. **Review BLOCKING-1 fix:** `add-task`/`add-from-spec` gain a **`--workflow` arg (default `sub-task`)** and record it (M7 hardcoded `single-task`), so the equality guard is genuine, not a tautology that would reject every real spawn.
- **Write→join seam = full edit-staging.** `copy_in` wired into a production `--task`-scoped edit path (copy-on-first-touch), so `created` *and* `edited-from-base` — and therefore `same-doc-clash` — are reachable from the real binary. The write-time `--task` barrier scopes each write to its own sub-area (the write-time complement to M7's join-time isolation check). *Why:* the human chose the full path over create-only; it closes the #1 integration seam through real writes, not fixtures.
- **5th emit class = `Spawn: `** — a reserved line-start marker mirroring `Run: `, one per fanned id, rendered through the adapter spawn template; shadow-checked by `workflow-refs.spawn-marker-not-shadowed`. Plus `workflow-refs.fan-out-join-paired` (every `fan-out` needs a matching `join` step kind). Inventory grows **18 → 20 checks / 11 → 13 intrinsic** ([validation.md](design/validation.md#mvp-check-inventory)). The `fan-out:`/`join:` step front-matter must be **parsed-and-honored** (pre-M8 `load_step_def` silently strips it → inert green prose).
- **Acceptance = two halves, neither faking spawn.** (A) an automated `cargo test`: deterministic N-`Spawn:` emit + the test invoking `jigc workflow --task` as N separate processes through the *real* write→join, byte-identical finalize (+ render-and-execute the spawn template to guard the L1 landmine). (B) a **recorded orchestrator-driven genuine concurrent Task-tool spawn** as a milestone-completion audit artifact (tree-hash match vs A's golden + a blackboard-invariant witness), **not a CI gate**. *Why:* by the determinism boundary the launch is assistant-owned; a headless subagent cannot spawn the Task tool, so no automated gate can drive it — presenting A as the genuine proof is the hollow-spawn trap. New flow 10 in [worked-examples.md](design/worked-examples.md).
- **`finalize.fan-out.squash` default = `true`**; **install-time launch-template validation = full** with a *decidable* forbidden-pattern rule (single-line invocation; no `<<author:`/ATX-heading/`Run:`/`Spawn:`/`> ` markers; ≤1 newline) rather than a vague "looks like prose" heuristic.
- **Deferred (recorded in [decisions-pending.md](implementation/decisions-pending.md)):** the **never-started runtime backstop** — M8's only defence against a broken launch is install-time schema validation; a schema-valid template that misfires at runtime is an accepted, unsurfaced gap (a never-started sub-area contributes nothing to the join). **Fan-out resumption = restart-from-scratch** (already locked 2026-06-04, reconfirmed — not re-litigated).
- **Forward-looking harness review** confirmed the M7 lesson again: **no new principle #8 — three new faces of #4 (the adapter-rendered launch line is an emitted-bytes contract, execute it), #5 (a marker that parses but leaves compose output unchanged is parsed-but-ignored), #7 (drive a write through the barrier from the binary, attempt out-of-bounds)** + the structural fact that the genuine spawn is unprovable by any automated gate → the orchestrator-driven completion artifact. Folded into [increment-workflow.md](implementation/increment-workflow.md) (M8 faces), [milestone-completion-workflow.md](implementation/milestone-completion-workflow.md) (spawn-class artifact), and the e2e-tester/validator agent defs.
- **M8 is doctype-neutral** — adds a data-value root (`{{milestone.tasks}}`, item = bare sub-task id, mirrors `store`), step kinds, and pack data; no schema/relation/create-gate change. **storage.md worktree-orchestration open question** reconciled (settled: directory isolation, no worktrees).
- **Independent design review** (pre-decompose, `design-reviewer`): the design held on every axis it probed (per-sub-task/squash reconciliation, two-half acceptance honesty, check-scope pinning, cross-ref anchors all sound). Two blocking findings + five advisories, all baked before decompose: **BLOCKING-1** the sub-task-workflow mismatch (above, fixed via `add-task --workflow`); **BLOCKING-2** storage.md body still named `milestone-execution` as a worktree case (scrubbed — fan-out is the directory-isolated by-task-id join, never worktrees); advisories = provenance write-once/sticky-`created` keyed on base-membership, commit docs collision-safe by construction (`commit:<sub-id>` unique per area), the barrier scopes *staging* writes not `bind`'s committed reads, the forbidden-pattern rule keyed on ≤1 newline in the whole rendered template, and `milestone-execution`'s include list shown so `fan-out-join-paired` is visibly satisfiable.

## 2026-06-04 — M8 decomposition: 6 risk-first increments

Cut into six linear increments ([roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md) → Milestone 8 decomposition), risk-first along the dependency chain the review named: **(1)** the compose-side spine — `{{milestone.tasks}}` root + `fan-out`/`join` step kinds (parse-and-honor) + the `Spawn:` emit class + the two new `workflow-refs` checks, engine-testable in isolation; **(2)** the re-entry spine — `jigc workflow <W> --task` + `add-task --workflow` (default `sub-task`) + provision-on-first-entry; **(3)** the write→join seam (the #1 integration risk) — the write-time `--task` barrier + copy-on-first-touch (`edited-from-base` reachable from the binary), verified to feed the M7 join's preconditions with real writes; **(4)** the adapter spawn binding + install-time template validation (the L1 guard); **(5)** the `sub-task` + `milestone-execution` pack workflows + per-sub-task commits + the `finalize.fan-out.squash` knob; **(6)** the two-half flow-10 acceptance (automated N-process determinism + the recorded orchestrator-driven genuine spawn). Ordering dependencies: 1 gates the emit chain; 2 gates 3; 1–5 gate the acceptance; 4 (adapter) and the squash knob in 5 are independent of the spawn chain but sequenced before the acceptance that exercises them. The genuine-spawn proof (Half B of increment 6) is an **orchestrator-driven milestone-completion artifact**, not a per-increment gate — by the determinism boundary no automated gate can drive a real Task-tool spawn.

## 2026-06-04 — M8 Increment 1 planning: decomposition

Increment 1 — `{{milestone.tasks}}` root + `fan-out`/`join` step kinds + the `Spawn:` emit class (the compose-side spine) — cut into 5 ordered, single-concern tasks ([roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md#increment-1--milestonetasks-root--fan-outjoin-step-kinds--the-spawn-emit-class-the-compose-side-spine); design [workflow-dialect.md](design/workflow-dialect.md) → data-value roots / On-disk definition format / Emitted format, [validation.md](design/validation.md#mvp-check-inventory) → Severity inventory). The surface is **net-new in code**: `ComposeContext` (`data_value.rs`) carries `task`/`catalog`/`store`, the resolver rejects every other root as `workflow-refs.undeclared-root`, `Resolution` has the `Catalog`/`Store` collection leaves but no `milestone`; `StepDef { id, body }` (`compose.rs`) drops front-matter at `load_step_def` (`strip_optional_front_matter`) and `ComposedStep { id, body, continues }` carries no kind — a test (`compose.rs:2970`) **proves a `fan-out:` marker today loads as inert prose**; `emit_line` has no `Spawn:` class; `workflow_refs*` runs `find_run_shadow` + emit per composed step body, no fan-out/join awareness; `knobs.yaml` + `INTRINSIC_CHECK_KEYS` (`knobs.rs:50`, surfaced via `pack.rs::intrinsic_knobs_yaml` generated *from* that const) declare 18 keys / 11 floored. Serial spine: the `over:` source root first (T1), then the step kinds that read it (T2), then the emit class that consumes both (T3), then the two conformance checks the honored kinds make checkable (T4), then the determinism + #5-face acceptance over the whole spine (T5). *Why this order:* T2's `fan-out.over` resolves `{{milestone.tasks}}`, which is not a declared root until T1; T3's `Spawn:` emission needs both the resolved collection (T1) and the step's kind/`run` (T2); T4's `fan-out-join-paired` + `spawn-marker-not-shadowed` need the per-step kinds T2 threads through composition; T5 asserts the byte-identical-across-orders + marker-honored-vs-absent property of the assembled T1–T4 spine.

- **T1 — the `milestone` data-value root: a collection leaf mirroring `store`.** Add a `milestone: Vec<String>` field to `ComposeContext` (the id-sorted sub-task ids the CLI feeds from `TaskList::enumerate`; the resolver does no milestone I/O, the determinism boundary, exactly as `store`/`catalog` are fed) + a `Resolution::Milestone { ids: Vec<String> }` collection-leaf variant + a resolver arm: a **bare** `milestone.tasks` (one `.tasks` hop, no `#fragment`, no `@`) resolves to `Resolution::Milestone`; `milestone` alone, a `@` marker, a `#fragment`, or any other/further hop is a blocking `workflow-refs.milestone-not-navigable` conformance error (mirroring `store-not-navigable`); `milestone.*` is **no longer** `undeclared-root`. The bare-`tasks`-only shape (vs `store`'s arbitrary doctype hop) is the design's fixed `.tasks` leaf ([workflow-dialect.md](design/workflow-dialect.md) → data-value roots: "its `.tasks` resolves to the milestone's sub-task collection"). **Done:** an engine unit test asserts `milestone.tasks` against a ctx fed `["alpha-fix","zebra-fix"]` resolves to `Resolution::Milestone { ids: [...] }` with the ids verbatim; an empty milestone resolves to the empty collection (empty text, not a finding); each of `milestone`, `@milestone.tasks`, `milestone.tasks#x`, `milestone.tasks.y`, `milestone.other` is a blocking `workflow-refs.milestone-not-navigable` (the `.other` is undeclared-leaf, *not* `undeclared-root`); the existing `data_value_resolution_cases` test's `milestone.x`-as-undeclared-root example is retargeted to a still-undeclared root (e.g. `nonesuch.x`); gate green. Files: `crates/engine/src/data_value.rs`.
- **T2 — `fan-out`/`join` step kinds: `load_step_def` parses and honors step front-matter.** `StepDef` gains a `kind: StepKind` field — `StepKind::Plain` (no front-matter, or front-matter with neither marker) / `StepKind::FanOut { over: String, run: String }` (`over:` the verbatim `{{ milestone.tasks }}` placeholder text, `run:` the `workflow:<id>` target string) / `StepKind::Join`. `load_step_def` parses the optional `---`-fenced config-family YAML front-matter (reusing the `split_front_matter` recognizer the workflow loader already uses) into the kind, *honoring* it instead of stripping-and-discarding; the body stays the post-front-matter remainder byte-for-byte; non-UTF-8 stays the `workflow-refs.not-utf8` block, and malformed/conflicting marker front-matter (both `fan-out` and `join`; a `fan-out` missing `over`/`run`) is a blocking located conformance finding. The kind must reach the emit/check boundary, so it threads onto `ComposedStep` (a doc-elaboration pin: `ComposedStep` gains a `kind`, set from the originating `StepDef`; continuation prose leaves stay `Plain`). **Done:** an engine unit test asserts the `steps/implement-tasks.md` fixture front-matter (`fan-out: { over: "{{ milestone.tasks }}", run: workflow:sub-task }`) loads to `StepKind::FanOut { over, run }` with the body intact; `join: {}` loads to `StepKind::Join`; a plain step (no front-matter) loads to `StepKind::Plain`; a both-markers / malformed-`fan-out` front-matter returns a blocking located finding; the prior `loads_fan_out_marker_as_inert_prose`-style strip test (`compose.rs:2970`) is updated to assert the kind is now parsed, not discarded; gate green. Files: `crates/engine/src/compose.rs`.
- **T3 — the `Spawn:` 5th emit class: a `fan-out` step emits one ` Spawn: `<re-entry-cmd>` ` per id, in id order.** Composition emits a `StepKind::FanOut` step by resolving its `over:` placeholder against `ctx` to a `Resolution::Milestone` collection and emitting **one ` Spawn: `<cmd>` ` line per id in the collection's (already id-sorted) order**, where `<cmd>` is the **bare CLI re-entry payload** `jigc workflow <run-workflow> --task <sub-id>` — the `<run-workflow>` taken from the step's `run: workflow:<id>` target, stripped of the `workflow:` prefix (the adapter launch-template wrapper is **increment 4**, explicitly out here — roadmap bullet 3 "the CLI payload; the adapter launch wrapper is increment 4"). A `fan-out` step whose `over:` resolves to a non-`Milestone`/empty result, or whose collection is empty, emits no `Spawn:` lines (empty is not a finding — the empty-milestone case); a `join`/`Plain` step emits as today. **Done:** an engine compose test composes a fixture workflow (`fan-out` step over `{{milestone.tasks}}`, `run: workflow:sub-task`, + a `join` step) against a ctx fed two sub-task ids and asserts the emitted text contains exactly ` Spawn: `jigc workflow sub-task --task alpha-fix` ` and ` Spawn: `jigc workflow sub-task --task zebra-fix` ` in id order and no others; an empty milestone composes the fan-out step to zero `Spawn:` lines; the `Spawn:` line matches the same strict line-start/backtick pattern `Run:` uses (machine-extractable); gate green. Files: `crates/engine/src/compose.rs`.
- **T4 — the two new `workflow-refs` checks (intrinsic, floored) + their `knobs.yaml` keys.** Add **`spawn-marker-not-shadowed`** (a `find_spawn_shadow` mirroring `find_run_shadow`: a step's *unresolved prose* must not start a line with the composer-reserved ` Spawn: ` marker; run per composed step body inside every `workflow_refs*` gate alongside the run-shadow check) and **`fan-out-join-paired`** (a workflow-level check over the composition's per-step kinds: a `fan-out` with no later `join` step, or a `join` with no preceding `fan-out`, is a blocking located conformance finding — uses the T2 kinds on `ComposedStep`). Declare both keys `validation.workflow-refs.spawn-marker-not-shadowed.severity` + `validation.workflow-refs.fan-out-join-paired.severity` (`floor: blocking`) in the embedded `knobs.yaml`, add them to `INTRINSIC_CHECK_KEYS`, update the `knobs.yaml` header comment **17 → 20 checks** (inventory 18 → 20 / 11 → 13 intrinsic per [validation.md](design/validation.md#mvp-check-inventory)), and refresh the `embedded_pack_config_declares_the_knob_surface` snapshot. **Done:** engine unit tests assert a step whose prose shadows ` Spawn: ` is a blocking `workflow-refs.spawn-marker-not-shadowed` located at that line, while a *genuine* fan-out step (whose `Spawn:` lines are composer-emitted, never authored prose) is clean; a workflow with a `fan-out` and no `join` (and vice-versa) is a blocking `workflow-refs.fan-out-join-paired`, while a paired `fan-out`+`join` is clean; `load_knobs` over the embedded `knobs.yaml` accepts the two new floored keys and the load-time intrinsic-floored assertion passes for the 13-key set; gate green incl. the refreshed snapshot. Files: `crates/engine/src/compose.rs`, `crates/engine/src/knobs.rs`, `crates/cli/pack/config/knobs.yaml`, `crates/cli/src/pack.rs` (snapshot).
- **T5 — determinism (#7) + the #5 face: byte-identical N-`Spawn:` across divergent orders; marker honored vs absent.** The acceptance over the assembled T1–T4 spine. **(#7)** the same milestone id *set* fed to the fan-out compose under **≥2 deliberately-divergent orders (id-order and its reverse, minimum)** yields **byte-identical** composed output — the id-sort lives at the `TaskList::enumerate`/resolver boundary, so neither feed order nor any map iteration reaches the emitted `Spawn:` directives; the test feeds the reverse order and asserts equal bytes. **(#5 face)** a `fan-out` step composes **differently** than the *same step with the marker absent* (the marker-present body emits N `Spawn:` directives; the marker-absent body — identical prose, no `fan-out:` front-matter — emits the prose inert with zero `Spawn:` lines), proving the marker is **honored, not parsed-but-ignored**. **Done:** an engine compose test asserts (a) composing the fan-out workflow with the milestone ctx fed `[zebra-fix, alpha-fix]` produces output **byte-identical** to the same fed `[alpha-fix, zebra-fix]`, with the `Spawn:` lines in id order in both; (b) the same step body with the `fan-out:` front-matter removed composes to output containing **zero** `Spawn:` lines and differing from the marker-present output; gate green. Files: `crates/engine/src/compose.rs`.

No new fork surfaced: the increment is fully covered by the milestone's settled decisions (2026-06-04 M8 planning entry — the `Spawn:` 5th emit class as a reserved line-start marker, **one per fanned id**, carrying the `jigc workflow <W> --task <id>` re-entry, the **adapter wrapper deferred to increment 4** so inc-1 emits the bare CLI payload; `fan-out:`/`join:` front-matter **parsed-and-honored** not stripped; `workflow-refs.spawn-marker-not-shadowed` + `fan-out-join-paired` intrinsic, inventory 18 → 20 / 11 → 13; M8 **doctype-neutral** — adds a data-value root + step kinds + pack data, no schema/relation change) and the locked [workflow-dialect.md](design/workflow-dialect.md) (data-value roots, On-disk definition format, Emitted format) / [validation.md](design/validation.md) (Severity inventory). **All five *Grouped scope* bullets map to a task:** bullet 1 *the `milestone` root mirroring `store`'s collection-leaf shape* → **T1**; bullet 2 *`fan-out`/`join` step kinds, `load_step_def` parses-and-honors* → **T2**; bullet 3 *the `Spawn:` 5th emit class* → **T3**; bullet 4 *the two `workflow-refs` checks + their `knobs.yaml` keys + the inventory bump* → **T4**; bullet 5 *determinism #7 + the #5 face* → **T5** (with the marker-honored-vs-inert split also exercised in T3's own done-criterion, the determinism+absent-marker acceptance consolidated in T5). The re-entry verb (`jigc workflow <W> --task`), `add-task --workflow`, the write-time barrier, copy-on-first-touch, the adapter spawn binding, the pack workflows, and the genuine spawn stay out — they are increments 2–6, not narrowed here. **Verified-fact grounding the executor must treat as red-step assumptions, not trust blindly:** (a) the `Spawn:` payload is the **bare** `jigc workflow <run> --task <id>`, *not* the adapter-rendered launch — the adapter render is increment 4 (roadmap bullet 3); a test that asserts an adapter-wrapped line here is wrong-scope; (b) `load_step_def` **today strips** the marker (`compose.rs:2970` proves it), so T2 is net-new parse-and-honor, never "wire an existing parsed kind"; (c) `ComposeContext`'s `undeclared-root` arm **today rejects `milestone`** and a live test (`data_value.rs` `data_value_resolution_cases`) *relies on `milestone.x` being undeclared* — T1 must retarget that test, the milestone root is genuinely net-new. Doc-elaboration pins the executor makes (not forks): (i) the `StepKind` enum shape + that the kind threads onto `ComposedStep` (set from the originating `StepDef`, continuation leaves `Plain`), golden-locked where bytes matter; (ii) the `Resolution::Milestone { ids }` variant name + the `milestone-not-navigable` check-id (mirroring `store-not-navigable`); (iii) the `run: workflow:<id>` → `<id>` strip and the exact `jigc workflow <id> --task <sub-id>` payload byte form, golden-locked; (iv) the `spawn-marker-not-shadowed` finding parallels `find_run_shadow` (per-body, located at the shadowing line) and `fan-out-join-paired` is a workflow-level kind-pairing check emitting a located finding with a route.

## 2026-06-04 — M8 Increment 1 T2 landed: `fan-out`/`join` step kinds parsed and honored

T2 landed (2026-06-04): `load_step_def` now **parses-and-honors** step front-matter instead of stripping-and-discarding (the dead `strip_optional_front_matter` removed). New `StepKind` enum — `Plain` / `FanOut { over, run }` / `Join` — internally serde-tagged (`tag = "kind"`, kebab-case, mirroring `CommandArg`) for an unambiguous golden discriminant; `StepDef` gains `kind`, `ComposedStep` gains `kind` (pin (i): set from the originating `StepDef`, **only the boundary leaf** inherits the kind; continuation prose leaves stay `Plain`). `over:` carries the verbatim `{{ milestone.tasks }}` text, `run:` the `workflow:sub-task` string. A new `parse_step_kind` over the front-matter YAML emits **`workflow-refs.step-kind-malformed`** (the doc-elaboration check-id the planner left unnamed — blocking, located at line 2, the front-matter) for: malformed YAML, **both** markers present, or a `fan-out` missing/empty `over` or `run`; front-matter declaring neither marker is `Plain`. `MapSource` test helper + the CLI `FillStepSource` thread the kind through (the fill pass rewrites only the body). The `compose.rs` strip-to-inert-prose test updated to assert the kind is now parsed. No new fork — within the planning pin's doc-elaboration latitude.

## 2026-06-04 — M8 Increment 2 planning: decomposition

Increment 2 — `jigc workflow <W> --task <id>` re-entry + `add-task --workflow` + sub-area provisioning (the re-entry spine) — cut into **3 ordered, single-concern tasks** ([roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md#increment-2--jigc-workflow-w---task-id-re-entry--add-task---workflow--sub-area-provisioning-the-re-entry-spine); design [write-commands.md](design/write-commands.md) → Minting a milestone (the `--workflow` arg), Sub-agent re-entry (`jigc workflow <W> --task <id>` + the equality guard + provision-on-first-entry)). Grounded in the current code: `engine::milestone::add_task`/`add_from_spec` already take a `workflow_id` param but the CLI hardcodes `SUB_TASK_WORKFLOW = "single-task"` (`crates/cli/src/milestone.rs:47`); `state::mint_task` already persists the workflow id (`state.rs:353`, read back by `read_workflow_id`); there is **no `jigc workflow` verb** (the top-level `Command` enum has `Start`/`Doc`/`Task`/`Config`/`Milestone`/`Setup`/`Upgrade`, no `Workflow`); `start --task` resume (`resume_in_repo`) composes the task's **own recorded** workflow with no `<W>` argument and no equality assertion; `add_task` mints the sub-area with `base`/`intent`/`workflow` but **no `docs/`** (`compose_core` provisions the commit doc only on the fresh `creates-task` mint path via `provision_commit_doc`, never on resume). Serial spine: the recorded `--workflow` value first (T1 — the guard's left-hand side), then the re-entry verb that composes `<W>` + asserts equality (T2), then the provision-on-first-entry the re-entry hangs (T3). *Why this order:* T2's equality guard compares `<W>` against the value `add-task` records, so T1 (the recorded value, default `sub-task`, replacing the hardcoded `single-task`) is the precondition that makes the guard genuine and not a tautology that rejects every real spawn (the review's BLOCKING-1 fix); T3 provisions on the re-entry path T2 introduces.

**Scope-critical fact the cut is honest about (a settled sequencing fact, not a fork):** the `sub-task` *pack workflow* does **not** ship until increment 5 (`crates/cli/pack/workflows/` has only `single-task`/`router`/`quick-fix`/`plan`/`implement-from-spec`), yet the `--workflow` **default** is `sub-task` per the roadmap. So T1 records `sub-task` faithfully as the default, but the **end-to-end re-entry+provision proof through the binary** (T2/T3 done-criteria) uses a **real existing `creates-task: true` workflow — `single-task`** — as the explicit `<W>` (the path the equality guard accepts and `provision_commit_doc` provisions); a `jigc workflow sub-task --task <id>` against the default-recorded value resolves the recorded id, reads the pack, and surfaces the existing `workflow-refs.unknown-workflow` block until increment 5 ships `sub-task` — which is the correct fail-loud behaviour, exercised as the unknown-workflow arm. This is not a scope reduction: bullet 1 (the `--workflow` arg + default + recording) is fully delivered in T1; the end-to-end compose proof rides a real workflow because the pack `sub-task` workflow is increment 5's scope by the roadmap's own sequencing.

- **T1 — `add-task`/`add-from-spec` gain `--workflow <id>` (default `sub-task`), recorded in the sub-task area.** Add a `--workflow <id>` clap arg (default `"sub-task"`) to `MilestoneCommand::AddTask` and `AddFromSpec`; thread it through `run_add_task`/`run_add_from_spec` to the engine's existing `workflow_id` param, **removing** the hardcoded `SUB_TASK_WORKFLOW = "single-task"` constant (`milestone.rs:47`). The recorded value lands in the sub-task area's `workflow` file via the existing `mint_task` → `read_workflow_id` path (no engine change — the param already exists; this only stops the CLI hardcoding it). **Done:** a CLI integration test (`crates/cli/tests/milestone.rs`) asserts `jigc milestone add-task <m> "<intent>"` with **no `--workflow`** records `sub-task` in `.jigc/tasks/<sub>/workflow` (default), and `--workflow single-task` records `single-task`; a clap unit test (`cli.rs`) asserts the arg parses with its default and an explicit value, for both `add-task` and `add-from-spec`; gate green. Files: `crates/cli/src/milestone.rs`, `crates/cli/src/cli.rs`. ([write-commands.md](design/write-commands.md) → Minting a milestone: "M8 gives both a `--workflow <id>` argument (default `sub-task`) … and records it".)
- **T2 — `jigc workflow <W> --task <id>` clap verb + dispatch: compose `<W>` for the sub-task, with the W-equality guard.** Add a top-level `Command::Workflow { workflow: String, task: String }` (`--task <id>` required; the `<W>` positional) and a dispatch arm; add a CLI compose function (e.g. `start::reenter_in_repo(cwd, workflow_id, task_id)` or a sibling in a re-entry module) that: resolves the sub-task working area (`.jigc/tasks/<id>/`, reject `no task <id>` if absent — mirroring `resume_in_repo`), reads the recorded workflow id (`read_workflow_id`), **asserts `<W>` equals it** (mismatch → a blocking routed finding, `fails loudly on a stale template`), verifies the base pin still matches HEAD (the same pinned-base guard `resume_in_repo` enforces), then composes `<W>` over the sub-task's persisted state (intent, bound roles) — reusing the resume compose machinery (`build_context` + the fill-aware `workflow-refs` gate + `compose_with_store`). This is **distinct from `jigc start --task`** (which composes the task's own recorded workflow with no `<W>` arg / no equality assertion). **No provisioning here** — compose/read path only (T3 adds provisioning). **Done:** a CLI integration test asserts (a) `jigc workflow single-task --task <sub>` (where the sub-task was `add-task --workflow single-task`) composes the `single-task` view (asserting a `single-task`-distinctive emitted line) and exits 0; (b) `jigc workflow router --task <sub>` (a `<W>` ≠ the recorded `single-task`) is **rejected** with a blocking finding naming the mismatch and exits non-zero; (c) `jigc workflow single-task --task <unknown>` rejects `no task`; (d) `jigc workflow sub-task --task <sub>` against a default-`sub-task`-recorded sub-task surfaces the existing `workflow-refs.unknown-workflow` block (the fail-loud arm until increment 5 ships the `sub-task` pack workflow); a clap unit test asserts `jigc workflow <W> --task <id>` parses and that `--task` is required; gate green. Files: `crates/cli/src/cli.rs`, `crates/cli/src/start.rs` (or a new `crates/cli/src/reenter.rs`). ([write-commands.md](design/write-commands.md) → Sub-agent re-entry: "composes the explicitly-named sub-workflow `<W>` … re-entry asserts `<W>` equals [the recorded workflow] (a mismatch is rejected)".)
- **T3 — provision-on-first-entry: the first `jigc workflow <W> --task <id>` provisions the sub-workflow's deterministic instances into `tasks/<sub>/docs/`.** Extend T2's re-entry path so that on entry it provisions the composed sub-workflow's workflow-provisioned instances (the commit doc, etc.) into the sub-area — reusing the existing `provision_commit_doc`/`fillable_form` provisioning logic (today private to `start.rs`, gated on `def.creates_task`), deferred to **first** entry rather than at `add-task`. Provisioning is **idempotent / first-entry-only**: a second re-entry of an already-provisioned area must not overwrite or duplicate the staged doc (so a sub-agent's in-progress edits survive a re-entry) — keyed on the doc's absence in `docs/` (mirroring how `provision_commit_doc` writes the empty skeleton once at mint). An unspawned sub-area (never re-entered) is **never provisioned** (the design's "areas that are never spawned are never provisioned"). **Done:** a CLI integration test asserts (a) after `jigc milestone add-task <m> "<intent>" --workflow single-task`, the sub-area has **no `docs/`**; (b) the first `jigc workflow single-task --task <sub>` creates `.jigc/tasks/<sub>/docs/commit:<sub>.md` as the fillable empty form (the same shape `jigc start` provisions at mint, asserted against the provisioned bytes); (c) a `creates-task: false` `<W>` (were one recorded) provisions nothing; (d) re-entering a second time after a simulated edit to the staged doc leaves the edited bytes intact (provisioning is first-entry-only, not re-run); (e) a never-re-entered sibling sub-area still has no `docs/`; gate green. Files: `crates/cli/src/start.rs` (or the re-entry module), `crates/cli/src/cli.rs`. ([write-commands.md](design/write-commands.md) → Sub-agent re-entry: "The first `jigc workflow <W> --task <id>` re-entry provisions the sub-workflow's deterministic instances … mirroring how `jigc start` provisions at mint time".)

No new fork surfaced: the increment is fully covered by the milestone's settled decisions (2026-06-04 M8 planning entry — the re-entry verb `jigc workflow <W> --task <id>` explicit + asserted-equal to the recorded mint workflow + provisions on first entry; `add-task`/`add-from-spec` gain `--workflow` default `sub-task` and record it, the BLOCKING-1 fix that makes the guard genuine; the finalize-less `sub-task` pack workflow + the write-time barrier + copy-on-first-touch are increments 5 and 3, **out of scope here**) and the locked [write-commands.md](design/write-commands.md) (Minting a milestone, Sub-agent re-entry). **All three *Grouped scope* bullets map to a task:** bullet 1 *`add-task`/`add-from-spec` gain `--workflow` (default `sub-task`), recorded, replacing the hardcoded `single-task`* → **T1**; bullet 2 *the `jigc workflow` verb + dispatch + the W-equality guard, distinct from `jigc start --task`* → **T2**; bullet 3 *provision-on-first-entry* → **T3**. **Out of this increment (increments 3–6, not narrowed here):** the write-time `--task` barrier + `copy_in`/copy-on-first-touch + `edited-from-base` provenance (increment 3); the adapter spawn binding + install-time template validation (increment 4); the `sub-task`/`milestone-execution` pack workflows + per-sub-task commits + `finalize.fan-out.squash` (increment 5); the genuine two-half acceptance (increment 6). **Verified-fact grounding the executor must treat as red-step assumptions, not trust blindly:** (a) `engine::milestone::add_task`/`add_from_spec` **already accept a `workflow_id` param** (`milestone.rs:190,253`) and `mint_task` **already persists it** (`state.rs:353`) — T1 is *un-hardcoding the CLI*, not adding engine plumbing; a test that asserts the recorded value through `read_workflow_id` proves the thread, never assume it; (b) there is **no `jigc workflow` verb today** — T2 is a net-new top-level `Command` arm, and `start --task` (`resume_in_repo`) is a *different* path (no `<W>`, no equality assertion) the guard must not be confused with; (c) the `sub-task` *pack workflow* is **absent until increment 5**, so the end-to-end compose/provision proof must use a real existing `creates-task: true` workflow (`single-task`) as `<W>` — a done-criterion asserting `jigc workflow sub-task --task …` composes *successfully* would be wrong-scope (it must instead assert the fail-loud `unknown-workflow` block); (d) `provision_commit_doc` is **gated on `def.creates_task`** and provisions only the `commit` doc (`FALLBACK_TYPE`) — T3 reuses it on first entry, so a `creates-task: false` `<W>` provisions nothing. Doc-elaboration pins the executor makes (not forks): (i) the `Command::Workflow` arm shape (`{ workflow: String, task: String }`, `--task` required) + whether the re-entry compose lives in `start.rs` or a new `reenter.rs` module; (ii) the mismatch finding's check-id (e.g. `workflow.reentry-mismatch` or reusing a `workflow-refs.*` id — name it, locate it on `task:<id>`, route it to "re-run with the recorded workflow `<recorded>`"); (iii) the first-entry-only provisioning predicate (absence of the `docs/<commit-addr>.md` skeleton, mirroring the mint-time write-once); (iv) whether T2/T3 reuse `resume_in_repo`'s body via extraction or add a sibling function — both within latitude provided the resume path's behaviour is unchanged.

## 2026-06-04 — M8 Increment 2 T1 landed: `add-task`/`add-from-spec` gain `--workflow` (default `sub-task`)

T1 landed (2026-06-04): the CLI no longer hardcodes the sub-task minting workflow. `MilestoneCommand::AddTask`/`AddFromSpec` gain a `--workflow <id>` clap arg (`#[arg(default_value = DEFAULT_SUB_TASK_WORKFLOW)]`, the constant renamed from `SUB_TASK_WORKFLOW = "single-task"` to `DEFAULT_SUB_TASK_WORKFLOW = "sub-task"` — the BLOCKING-1 fix flipping the default so the inc-2 equality guard is genuine, not a tautology); threaded through `run_add_task`/`run_add_from_spec` into the engine's existing `workflow_id` param (no engine change — `add_task`/`add_from_spec` already accept it, `mint_task` already persists it). A CLI integration test drives the binary and reads `.jigc/tasks/<sub>/workflow` (the `read_workflow_id` companion) proving the thread: no `--workflow` records `sub-task`, `--workflow single-task` records `single-task`. No new fork — within the planning pin's latitude.

## 2026-06-04 — M8 Increment 2 T2 landed: `jigc workflow <W> --task <id>` re-entry verb + W-equality guard

T2 landed (2026-06-04): the net-new top-level **`Command::Workflow { workflow: String, task: String }`** (pin (i): `--task` required, `<W>` positional; re-entry compose lives in `start.rs` alongside resume, not a new module — `reenter_in_repo` shares the extracted `compose_task_workflow` spine with `resume_in_repo`, so the resume path's behaviour is unchanged). Dispatch → `run_reenter` → `start::reenter_in_repo(cwd, <W>, <id>)`, which: resolves `.jigc/tasks/<id>/` (reject `no task <id>` if absent), verifies the base pin against HEAD (same pinned-base guard as resume), then runs the **W-equality guard** — `<W>` must equal the sub-task's recorded mint workflow (`read_workflow_id`), else a blocking routed finding (pin (ii): check-id **`workflow-refs.workflow-mismatch`**, message naming `<W>` + the recorded id + the sub-task, route "re-run as `jigc workflow <recorded> --task <id>`"). On a passing guard it composes `<W>` over the sub-task's persisted intent + bound roles via the shared spine (build_context + fill-aware `workflow-refs` gate + `compose_with_store`). The shared spine now reads the workflow body through `read_workflow` (the routed `workflow-refs.unknown-workflow` mapper, replacing the prior generic `read_pack` in the resume path — equivalent for a shipped id, but a recorded-yet-unshipped id like `sub-task` before increment 5 now surfaces the proper fail-loud block). **No provisioning** — read/compose only (T3 adds it). A CLI integration test drives the binary on a milestone with two sub-tasks and asserts all four done-criterion cases (compose single-task / mismatch-reject / no-task / `sub-task` unknown-workflow); a clap unit test asserts the verb parses with `--task` required. No new fork — within the planning pin's doc-elaboration latitude (pins (i),(ii),(iv)).

## 2026-06-04 — M8 Increment 2 T3 landed: provision-on-first-entry for `jigc workflow <W> --task <id>`

M8 Inc 3 T2 landed (2026-06-04): the write-time barrier — every `jigc doc set-field`/`set-slot` resolves its staged-doc destination through `staged_path(task_dir, address, task_id)`, which **refuses up front** any destination that lexically lands **outside** `tasks/<id>/docs/` (the slug is structural, not sanitized — a `../…` slug would otherwise escape into a sibling area). Pin (ii) resolved within latitude: the containment predicate lives in the **CLI** (`within_area` over a `lexically_normalize` that folds `.`/`..` syntactically — no on-disk `canonicalize`, since a refused destination does not exist); the area anchor is sourced from `state::instance_path(.., "_", "_").parent()` so the engine keeps owning the `docs/` layout constant. The finding is `write.area-barrier`, **blocking + located + routed** (mirrors the M7 `join.area-isolation` shape via `Finding::graded` + `Location::addressed`), emitted directly with no `knobs.yaml` row (the blocking-but-untunable precedent). The barrier scopes only `jigc doc` staging writes — `jigc task bind` stages nothing and is never barriered (verified-fact (e)). A CLI integration test (`doc_barrier.rs`) drives a real `jigc doc set-slot --task <subA>` whose slug path-escapes into subB's area and asserts non-zero exit + the barrier finding + nothing written under subB, alongside the in-area write succeeding; a unit test pins `within_area` (accepts the in-area path, rejects an escaping one). No new fork.

T3 landed (2026-06-04): the first re-entry now provisions the sub-workflow's deterministic commit doc into `tasks/<sub>/docs/commit:<sub>.md`, the deferred mirror of `jigc start`'s mint-time provisioning (`add_task` mints base/intent/workflow but **no** docs/; provisioning waits for first entry so unspawned sub-areas stay docs-less). Threaded a `provision: bool` through the shared `compose_task_workflow` spine: re-entry (`reenter_in_repo`) passes `true`, top-level resume (`resume_in_repo`) passes `false` (its task was already provisioned at mint). The new `provision_on_first_entry` is **`creates-task`-gated** (a `creates-task: false` `<W>` — the router — provisions nothing, mirroring the no-task compose arm) and **idempotent / first-entry-only**, keyed on the *absence* of the `docs/commit:<id>.md` skeleton (the mint-time write-once), so a sub-agent's in-progress edits survive a second re-entry. Reuses the existing `provision_commit_doc`/`fillable_form` (refactored to take `dir + id` instead of `&MintedTask`, so the mint path and re-entry path share one provisioner). A CLI integration test asserts all five done-criterion cases (no docs/ after add-task / first re-entry provisions byte-identical to a top-level `jigc start --workflow single-task` mint of the same intent / `creates-task:false` provisions nothing / a second re-entry after an edit leaves the edited bytes intact / a never-re-entered sibling stays docs-less). No new fork.

## 2026-06-04 — M8 Increment 3 planning: decomposition

Increment 3 — the write-time `--task` barrier + copy-on-first-touch (the write→join seam, the #1 integration risk) — cut into **6 ordered, single-concern tasks** ([roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md#increment-3--the-write-time---task-barrier--copy-on-first-touch-the-writejoin-seam-the-1-integration-risk); design [write-commands.md](design/write-commands.md) → The write-time `--task`-scoped barrier / copy-on-first-touch / Sub-agent re-entry, [storage.md](design/storage.md#the-by-task-id-join-m7) → classification by provenance / Same-doc clash). Grounded in the current code: `jigc doc` exposes **only** `Create` / `SetField` / `SetSlot` (`doc.rs:36-63` — there is **no `jigc doc add-item` verb**; `engine::write::add_item` exists at `write.rs:1189` but is never CLI-exposed); `ActiveTask::resolve` (`doc.rs:187-208`) has **no `--task` selector** and `bail!`s on >1 task; the edit verbs `read_staged` an instance and **reject if absent** (`doc.rs:270-274`) — there is **no copy-on-first-touch in the edit path**, and `engine::state::copy_in` (`state.rs:218`, → `Provenance::EditedFromBase`) has **zero production callers**; `ProvenanceRecord::record` is **last-write** (`state.rs:143`, `insert` overwrites) — *not* write-once/sticky; `provision_doc` records `Created` (`state.rs:202`) but `provision_commit_doc` (`start.rs:80-89`) **persists the commit doc via `state::persist` directly, bypassing provenance entirely** (it records **no** `created` entry today — net-new, not reuse); the committed body the copy-in must source is `<location>/<slug>.md` (resolved via `schema.location`, as `committed_store` `start.rs:1027`); the M7 join (`milestone.rs:519`) consumes each sub-area's `docs/<addr>.md` + `provenance.json` (via `ProvenanceRecord::load`), and flow-9 (`flow9_milestone_join.rs:179` `stage_doc`) **hand-stages** those two inputs "because no front-door verb yet stages into a milestone sub-area." Serial spine: the `--task` selector first (T1 — the area every write must name), then the barrier that scopes a write to that area (T2), then the write-once/sticky provenance the join's clash rule depends on (T3), then `copy_in` wired into the production edit path (T4 — depends on T3's provenance + T1/T2's `--task` path), then the #7-barrier face (T5), then the seam-verification e2e replacing flow-9's hand-staging (T6). *Why this order:* a write can't be scoped (T2) before it can name its area (T1); the barrier (T2) and provenance (T3) must be correct before `copy_in` rides the edit path (T4) so the first-touch copy records the right bit through the right barrier; T5/T6 exercise the assembled T1–T4 seam from the binary (T5 the barrier face, T6 the full real-write→join seam).

- **T1 — the `--task <id>` selector on `jigc doc create`/`set-field`/`set-slot`; explicit-wins active-task resolution.** Add an optional `--task <id>` to each `DocCommand` variant (or as a shared `doc`-level arg threaded into each `run_*`), and rework `ActiveTask::resolve(cwd)` → `resolve(cwd, task: Option<&str>)`: **explicit `--task <id>` wins** (resolve `<repo>/.jigc/tasks/<id>/`, reject `no task <id>` if absent — mirroring `start::reenter_in_repo`'s no-task reject); **else the single active task**; **else (zero, or >1 with no `--task`) reject** with the existing routes (`no active task — start one with jigc start` / `more than one active task — name one with --task <id>`). No barrier yet (T2) and no copy-in (T4) — pure selection. **Done:** a CLI integration test (`crates/cli/tests/doc_write.rs` or a new `doc_task_scope.rs`) asserts, on a repo with **two** milestone sub-task areas each carrying a provisioned `commit:<sub>` doc, that `jigc doc set-slot commit:<subA>#summary --from-file - --task <subA>` (stdin) edits **subA's** staged doc and leaves subB's untouched; `--task <subB>` targets subB; **no** `--task` with two areas present rejects `more than one active task`; `--task <unknown>` rejects `no task <unknown>`; a clap unit test asserts `--task` parses (optional) on all three verbs; gate green. Files: `crates/cli/src/doc.rs`, `crates/cli/src/cli.rs`. *(Scope-honesty note carried to T2: roadmap bullet 1 lists `add-item` among the `--task`-gaining verbs, but **no `jigc doc add-item` CLI verb exists** — the selector mechanism is built generically over the three shipped verbs and would cover `add-item` the day it ships in a later increment; nothing is withheld. This is a vacuous clause, not a reduction — the enforceable substance, selector + resolution + barrier, is fully delivered T1–T2.)* ([write-commands.md](design/write-commands.md) → The write-time `--task`-scoped barrier: "explicit `--task <id>` wins; else the single active task; else … reject".)
- **T2 — the write-time barrier: a staged-doc destination outside `tasks/<id>/` is refused at write-time.** With the area resolved (T1), the barrier asserts the **staged-doc destination path** of every `create`/`set-field`/`set-slot`/copy-in lands **inside the resolved `tasks/<id>/docs/`** — a path that would escape it (`..`, an absolute address, a slug resolving outside the area) is a **blocking located finding** routed to a human, refused **up front, never deferred to the join** (the write-time complement to M7's join-time isolation check). `jigc task bind` is untouched (it is a `jigc task` verb, not `jigc doc`, and stages nothing — the barrier never applies to its committed-store reads). **Done:** a CLI integration test asserts a `jigc doc` write whose resolved destination would land outside the named `tasks/<id>/docs/` is **refused** (non-zero exit, a blocking finding, **nothing written** outside the area) while a within-area write of the same verb succeeds; an engine/CLI unit test asserts the destination-containment predicate (`canonicalized dest starts_with tasks/<id>/docs/`) accepts the in-area path and rejects an escaping one; gate green. Files: `crates/cli/src/doc.rs` (+ the containment helper, engine or CLI per the executor's pin). ([write-commands.md](design/write-commands.md) → The write-time `--task`-scoped barrier: "a `create`/`set-slot`/`set-field`/`add-item`/the copy-in whose **staged-doc destination path** would land outside `tasks/<id>/` is rejected at write-time".)
- **T3 — write-once, sticky-`created` provenance + `provision_commit_doc` records `created`.** Two engine corrections keyed on base-membership: (a) `ProvenanceRecord::record` becomes **write-once / sticky** — a `created` entry **never flips** to `edited-from-base` on a later same-area edit (a created-then-edited doc stays `created`); only the **first** touch of a slug records its bit, and `created` (absent at base) is sticky (mirror the design: provenance recorded **once, at first touch, keyed on whether the slug existed in the committed store at the milestone base**); (b) **`provision_commit_doc` records `created` provenance** — today (`start.rs:80-89`) it persists the commit doc via `state::persist` and writes **no** provenance entry, so a per-sub-task commit doc would be invisible to the join's manifest; route it through `provision_doc` (or add the `record_provenance(..,Created)` call) so `commit:<sub-id>` lands in the area's `provenance.json` (collision-safe by construction — `commit:<sub-id>` is unique per area, never reaching the suffix/clash rules). **Done:** an engine unit test asserts (i) `record(addr, Created)` then `record(addr, EditedFromBase)` leaves the manifest reading **`Created`** (sticky, write-once), while a first-and-only `record(addr, EditedFromBase)` reads `EditedFromBase`; (ii) after `provision_commit_doc`/`provision_on_first_entry` over a sub-area, `ProvenanceRecord::load` reports `commit:<id>` → `Created`; gate green. Files: `crates/engine/src/state.rs`, `crates/cli/src/start.rs`. ([storage.md](design/storage.md#the-by-task-id-join-m7) → classification by provenance; [write-commands.md](design/write-commands.md) → copy-on-first-touch: "Provenance is recorded once, at first touch, keyed on whether the slug existed in the committed store at the milestone base … `created` is sticky"; `provision_commit_doc` likewise records `created`.)
- **T4 — copy-on-first-touch: `copy_in` wired into the production `--task` edit path.** On a `set-field`/`set-slot` (`run_set_field`/`run_set_slot`) whose addressed slug is **absent from the sub-area's `docs/`** but **present in the committed store at the milestone base** (`<location>/<slug>.md` exists), the edit path **copies the committed body into the sub-area** via `state::copy_in` (→ `edited-from-base`, the first-touch canonicalize) **before** splicing — replacing today's hard `read_staged` "no staged instance … provision it first" reject for the base-existing case. A `create` stays `provision_doc` → `created` (T3); an address neither staged nor base-committed keeps the existing actionable reject. This is the **only** new production caller of `copy_in` (zero today). Reads of committed state are untouched. **Done:** a CLI integration test asserts (i) a `jigc doc set-slot <addr>#<slot> --task <sub>` against a slug that **exists in the committed store** (a committed adr at HEAD) but is **absent from the sub-area** copies the committed body in, splices, and the area's `provenance.json` records `<addr>` → `edited-from-base`; (ii) a second edit of the same doc keeps `edited-from-base` and does **not** re-copy (the staged copy is the source); (iii) a `create`d-then-edited doc stays `created` (T3's stickiness, end-to-end through the verb); (iv) an address neither staged nor committed still rejects; gate green. Files: `crates/cli/src/doc.rs`. ([write-commands.md](design/write-commands.md) → copy-on-first-touch: "The first write to such a doc **copies the committed body into the sub-area** (`copy_in` → `edited-from-base`), then splices".)
- **T5 — the #7-barrier face (hardening): drive a write through the barrier from the binary, attempt an out-of-bounds write.** The hardening-#7 face for M8 ([increment-workflow.md](implementation/increment-workflow.md) → M8 faces: "drive a write *through the barrier from the binary* … attempt a write *outside* the named sub-area … **never pre-stage the area**"). A CLI integration test, on a milestone with ≥2 provisioned sub-areas, drives a **real `jigc doc … --task <sub>`** write (never hand-staging the area first) and asserts it lands **only** inside `tasks/<sub>/docs/`; then attempts a write whose resolved destination would escape the named sub-area (e.g. an address/`--task` pairing whose destination would land in a **sibling** area or outside `tasks/`) and asserts it is **refused** (non-zero, blocking finding, the sibling/escape path **unchanged on disk**). **Done:** the test (a) provisions areas only via `jigc workflow … --task` (the T-of-inc-2 path) + the T1/T4 write path, never `fs::write` into `docs/`; (b) asserts the in-bounds write succeeds and the out-of-bounds attempt is refused with nothing written outside the named area; gate green. Files: `crates/cli/tests/` (a new `doc_barrier.rs` or extend `doc_task_scope.rs`). ([increment-workflow.md](implementation/increment-workflow.md) → M8 faces, #7 face.)
- **T6 — the seam verification: real `--task` writes feed the M7 join; `edited-from-base` × `created` overlap triggers `same-doc-clash`.** The integration-seam discipline ([roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md) bullet 3). A CLI integration test asserts a real `jigc workflow <W> --task <sub>` (inc-2 re-entry, first-entry provision) + `jigc doc … --task <sub>` (T1/T4) sequence over a milestone produces in each sub-area **exactly** the `docs/<addr>.md` bodies + a **conforming `provenance.json`** the M7 `milestone::join` consumes — i.e. the join runs over these real-write-staged areas (not `stage_doc` hand-staging) and merges/blocks correctly; and that a **real** overlap — one sub-area `edited-from-base` a base-committed slug while another `created` (or `edited-from-base`s) the **same** slug — triggers the existing `join.same-doc-clash` block through the binary. **Done:** the test (a) drives `jigc milestone create`/`add-task`/`jigc workflow --task`/`jigc doc --task` to populate ≥2 sub-areas with real writes (one `edited-from-base` via T4's copy-in over a committed doc, one `created`), runs `jigc milestone join`, and asserts the join consumes the real-written `provenance.json`+bodies (a disjoint case merges; the same-slug `edited-from-base`×`created` mixed case **blocks** `join.same-doc-clash`); (b) does **not** use the flow-9 `stage_doc` hand-staging helper for the staged inputs under test; gate green. Files: `crates/cli/tests/` (a new `flow9_seam.rs` or extend `flow9_milestone_join.rs`). ([roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md) bullet 3: "assert a real `jigc workflow … --task <id>` + `jigc doc … --task <id>` sequence produces exactly the `docs/<addr>.md` + conforming `provenance.json` the M7 join consumes — replacing flow-9's hand-staging — and that a real `edited-from-base` × `created` overlap triggers the existing `same-doc-clash` block".)

No new fork surfaced: the increment is fully covered by the milestone's settled decisions (2026-06-04 M8 planning entry — **write→join seam = full edit-staging**: `copy_in` wired into a production `--task`-scoped edit path, copy-on-first-touch, so `created` *and* `edited-from-base` — and therefore `same-doc-clash` — are reachable from the real binary; the write-time `--task` barrier scopes each write to its own sub-area; the advisory that **provenance is write-once / sticky-`created` keyed on base-membership** and that `commit:<sub-id>` commit docs are **collision-safe by construction**; the **#7 barrier face** — drive a write through the barrier from the binary, attempt out-of-bounds) and the locked [write-commands.md](design/write-commands.md) (The write-time `--task`-scoped barrier, copy-on-first-touch) / [storage.md](design/storage.md#the-by-task-id-join-m7) (classification by provenance, Same-doc clash). **All four *Grouped scope* bullets map to a task:** bullet 1 *the `--task` selector + active-task resolution + the barrier* → **T1** (selector + resolution) + **T2** (the barrier); bullet 2 *copy-on-first-touch (`copy_in` into production) + `provision_commit_doc` records `created` + write-once sticky-`created`* → **T3** (the write-once/sticky provenance + `provision_commit_doc` → `created`) + **T4** (`copy_in` wired into the edit path); bullet 3 *the seam verification* → **T6**; bullet 4 *the #7-barrier face* → **T5**. The `add-item` clause of bullet 1 is delivered vacuously — **no `jigc doc add-item` CLI verb exists** (`add_item` is engine-only), so the generically-built `--task` selector (T1) + barrier (T2) cover it the day a later increment ships the verb; nothing approved is withheld (this is a no-op clause, not a reduction, and is **not** silently dropped — it is tracked here). The adapter spawn binding (increment 4), the `sub-task`/`milestone-execution` pack workflows + per-sub-task commits + `finalize.fan-out.squash` (increment 5), and the genuine two-half acceptance (increment 6) stay out — not narrowed here. **Verified-fact grounding the executor must treat as red-step assumptions, not trust blindly:** (a) `provision_commit_doc` (`start.rs:80-89`) **records NO provenance today** — it persists via `state::persist`, bypassing `provision_doc`; T3's "records `created`" is **net-new**, a test must assert the `commit:<id>` → `created` entry appears, never assume it; (b) `ProvenanceRecord::record` (`state.rs:143`) is **last-write `insert`**, *not* write-once/sticky — T3 changes the semantics, a test must assert `Created` survives a later `EditedFromBase` record; (c) `state::copy_in` exists (`state.rs:218`) but has **zero production callers** — T4 is its first wiring into the edit path, the red step must prove the committed body is copied in + `edited-from-base` recorded through the *real verb*, never trust that the primitive "just works" in the new path; (d) **no `jigc doc add-item` verb exists** — a done-criterion asserting `add-item --task` would be wrong-scope; (e) the barrier applies only to **`jigc doc` staging writes**, **not** `jigc task bind` (a separate verb that stages nothing) — a test asserting `bind` is barriered is wrong-scope; (f) the M7 `milestone::join` already consumes `docs/<addr>.md`+`provenance.json` per sub-area (`milestone.rs:519`) — T6 feeds it real-write-staged areas, **not** the flow-9 `stage_doc` helper (`flow9_milestone_join.rs:179`), which the seam replaces. Doc-elaboration pins the executor makes (not forks): (i) whether `--task` rides as a per-variant clap arg or a shared `doc`-level arg, and the `ActiveTask::resolve(cwd, task)` signature; (ii) the barrier's check-id (e.g. `doc.write-out-of-area` / a `workflow-refs.*`-style id), located + routed, and whether the containment predicate lives in the engine or the CLI; (iii) the write-once/sticky predicate's exact shape in `ProvenanceRecord::record` (first-entry-wins vs created-sticky-only — both satisfy the design); (iv) the copy-in trigger predicate in the edit path (slug absent from `docs/` **and** `<location>/<slug>.md` exists at the area's base) and how the committed source bytes are located (reuse the `schema.location`-based committed-path resolution `committed_store`/`task.rs` already use); (v) the test-file homes (extend vs new `doc_task_scope.rs`/`doc_barrier.rs`/`flow9_seam.rs`).

## 2026-06-04 — M8 Increment 3 T3 landed: write-once sticky-`created` provenance + `provision_commit_doc` records `created`

T3 landed (2026-06-04): `ProvenanceRecord::record` is now **write-once** (pin (iii), first-entry-wins shape — `entry().or_insert`), replacing the last-write `insert`, so a `created` doc later copied-in (recorded `edited-from-base`) stays `created` — the join's mixed-case clash discriminator keys on base-membership, never on whether this area later edited the doc; a first-and-only `edited-from-base` reads back `edited-from-base`. `provision_commit_doc` (`start.rs`), which persists via `state::persist` (bypassing `provision_doc`), now records `commit:<id>` → `Created` via the new public `state::record_doc_provenance` (thin wrapper over the internal `record_provenance`), so the per-sub-task commit doc appears in the manifest the by-task-id join consumes — collision-safe by construction (`commit:<sub-id>` is sub-task-id-derived, unique per sub-area). Reached on both the mint path and the re-entry first-entry path; write-once keeps a re-entry from flipping it. Engine unit test asserts the sticky/write-once predicate (i); a CLI unit test drives `provision_on_first_entry` and asserts `ProvenanceRecord::load` reports `commit:<id>` → `Created`, idempotent on a second entry (ii). No new fork — within the planning pin's doc-elaboration latitude (pin (iii)).
