## 2026-06-04 — M8 Increment 3 T4 landed: copy-on-first-touch wired into the production `--task` edit path

T4 landed (2026-06-04): `state::copy_in` gains its **first production caller** — `ActiveTask::read_or_copy_in` (`doc.rs`), the staged-body read both `run_set_field` and `run_set_slot` now route through. Three ordered cases: (1) **already staged** → read the staged body (the steady-state path; a second edit therefore never re-copies, so a prior edit survives); (2) **absent from the area but committed at base** → `copy_in` the committed `<location>/<slug>.md` body (→ `edited-from-base`, recorded write-once by T3), then read the copy — replacing the old hard `read_staged` reject for the base-existing case; (3) **neither staged nor committed** → the unchanged `no staged instance … provision it first` reject. Pin (iv) resolved as planned: the copy-in trigger predicate is **slug absent from the area `docs/` AND `<location>/<slug>.md` exists at base**, and the committed source path reuses `engine::store::canonical_path` (the same `schema.location`-keyed resolution `committed_store`/`task::bind` use); the committed body is read from the working tree at that path (the established `committed_store`/`bind` read pattern — no `git show`). `ActiveTask` gains a `repo_root` field (the copy-in anchor). The committed source file is untouched — copy-in writes only the working copy. CLI integration test (`doc_copy_in.rs`) drives the real binary over a milestone sub-area + a committed adr at HEAD and asserts all four done-criterion cases: (i) first edit copies the committed body in (the other committed slices survive, the edited slice is spliced) + `provenance.json` records `adr:<slug>` → `edited-from-base` + the committed source is unchanged; (ii) a second edit keeps `edited-from-base` and does not re-copy (the first edit's prose survives); (iii) a created-then-edited doc stays `created` (T3 stickiness through the verb); (iv) an address neither staged nor committed still rejects. No new fork.

## 2026-06-05 — M8 Increment 4 planning: decomposition

Increment 4 — the adapter spawn binding + install-time template validation — cut into **4 ordered, single-concern tasks** ([roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md#increment-4--the-adapter-spawn-binding--install-time-template-validation); design [assistant-adapter.md](design/assistant-adapter.md) → Bind the spawn mechanism, The adapter profile). Grounded in the current code: `AdapterProfile` (`adapter.rs:43`) is `#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]` with **no `spawn` field** — a profile carrying `spawn:` **fails to parse** today (the model rejects it); the shipped `adapters/claude-code.yaml` carries no `spawn:` block (golden-pinned at `adapter.rs:503`); there is **no template-render helper** (no `{{workflow}}`/`{{task_id}}` substitution anywhere — net-new) and **no install-time template validation**; the single MVP install path is `setup::install` (`setup.rs:81`, four steps: reference floor → project-layer init → allowlist → hook), driven by `Command::Setup` (`cli.rs:217`) — **`jigc adapter install`/`adapter regenerate` are not distinct shipped verbs** (design line 20 makes `jigc setup` the entry point and "regenerate on upgrade"; no M8 prior increment added them); the `jigc workflow <W> --task <id>` re-entry verb **exists** (M8 inc 2, `cli.rs:92` → `run_reenter` → `start::reenter_in_repo`). Serial spine: the `spawn` field + render first (T1 — the surface the validator reads), then the decidable validation rule as a pure function with full unit coverage (T2), then wiring it into the install path so a broken profile fails install proven through the binary (T3), then the #4-face hardening — render through the CLI and execute the rendered bytes against the real verb (T4). *Why this order:* T2 validates a rendered template, which only exists once T1 ships the field + render; T3 wires T2's rule into `setup::install`, needing both; T4's render→extract→execute rides the T1 render + the shipped `jigc workflow … --task` verb and is the emitted-bytes (#4-face) capstone over the whole spine.

- **T1 — `AdapterProfile` gains a `spawn` field (parses under `deny_unknown_fields`) + the launch-template render.** Add a `spawn: SpawnTarget { template: String }` field to `AdapterProfile` (a new modelled struct so `deny_unknown_fields` accepts a `spawn:` block; an accessor `spawn()` mirroring `reference()`/`hook()`), add the `spawn:` block to the shipped `adapters/claude-code.yaml` (the canonical one-line template `Use your Task tool to run: `` `jigc workflow {{workflow}} --task {{task_id}}` ``, verbatim from [assistant-adapter.md](design/assistant-adapter.md) → The adapter profile), and add a **render fn** `render_spawn(template, workflow, task_id) -> String` doing the two-token **lexical** substitution (`{{workflow}}` → workflow, `{{task_id}}` → task_id — *not* the engine data-value placeholder grammar; these are plain template tokens per the design). The loader-surface golden (`claude_code_profile_bytes_are_canonical`) and the loader test (`claude_code_profile_loads_with_inject_and_allowlist_targets`) are updated to include the spawn block/field. **Done:** an `adapter.rs` unit test asserts (a) `load_profile("claude-code")` now exposes `profile.spawn().template` equal to the shipped one-line template (the field parses, no longer rejected by `deny_unknown_fields`); (b) `render_spawn(template, "sub-task", "alpha-fix")` returns the template with **both** tokens substituted (` `jigc workflow sub-task --task alpha-fix` ` present, no residual `{{workflow}}`/`{{task_id}}`); the refreshed bytes golden over `claude-code.yaml` includes the `spawn:` block; gate green. Files: `crates/cli/src/adapter.rs`, `crates/cli/adapters/claude-code.yaml`. ([assistant-adapter.md](design/assistant-adapter.md) → Bind the spawn mechanism: "The profile carries a launch template … the CLI renders its fan-out dispatch through the template"; The adapter profile: the `spawn: template:` block.)
- **T2 — the decidable install-time validation rule as a pure function, full unit coverage.** A pure fn `validate_spawn_template(template) -> Result<(), <typed reason>>` (engine-free of I/O — it operates on the **whole rendered template string**) asserting the **full decidable rule** from [assistant-adapter.md](design/assistant-adapter.md) → Bind the spawn mechanism, in one place: (1) **both placeholders present** — the *unrendered* template literally contains `{{task_id}}` **and** `{{workflow}}`; (2) **the invocation pattern present** — the template contains a `jigc workflow … --task …` invocation; (3) **the forbidden-pattern lexical rule over the whole template**: **≤1 newline total**, **exactly one backticked span**, and **no** `<<author:` marker, **no** ATX heading (`#`/`##` at a line start), **no** `Run:`/`Spawn:`/`> ` directive marker anywhere. Each failure carries a **precise pointer** to the violated clause (the install error's message). Purely lexical, decidable, no judgement. **Done:** `adapter.rs` unit tests assert the shipped template **passes**, and a table of broken templates each **fails on the expected clause**: missing `{{task_id}}`; missing `{{workflow}}`; no `jigc workflow … --task` invocation; two newlines; zero backtick spans; two backtick spans; a `<<author:` marker; an ATX heading line; a `Run:`/`Spawn:`/`> ` marker; gate green. Files: `crates/cli/src/adapter.rs`. ([assistant-adapter.md](design/assistant-adapter.md) → Bind the spawn mechanism: the three-part schema — required placeholders, required invocation pattern, the decidable forbidden-pattern rule.)
- **T3 — wire validation into the install path: a broken template fails install with a precise pointer, proven through the binary.** Call T2's `validate_spawn_template` inside `setup::install` (over the loaded profile's spawn template) so the install **does not complete** on a broken template — a single blocking `setup.*` finding (e.g. `setup.spawn-template`) carrying the precise clause pointer as its route, mapped to a non-zero exit by the dispatcher (the established `setup.*` block-payload envelope). The shipped profile (T1) passes, so a real `jigc setup` still succeeds. **Done:** a CLI/`setup.rs` test asserts (a) `install` over the **shipped** profile (valid spawn) succeeds (the existing success path is unbroken); (b) `install` over a profile whose spawn template **violates** the rule yields a **blocking `setup.spawn-template` finding with a route naming the violated clause** and the dispatcher exits non-zero; **and a CLI integration test through the real binary** (`tests/setup.rs`) drives `jigc setup` in a throwaway repo with the shipped (good) profile and asserts a clean install, plus — via a test that feeds **several rejected templates** through the install core — that each broken template fails install (the *Deliverable*'s "setup with a good template + several rejected ones"); gate green. Files: `crates/cli/src/setup.rs`, `crates/cli/tests/setup.rs`. ([assistant-adapter.md](design/assistant-adapter.md) → Bind the spawn mechanism: "Validation runs at `jigc setup` … A broken template is an install-time error with a precise pointer to the schema violation; the install does not complete".)
- **T4 — the #4 face (hardening): render the template through the CLI, execute the rendered command verbatim, assert it reaches the real `jigc workflow … --task` verb.** The hardening-#4 face for M8 ([increment-workflow.md](implementation/increment-workflow.md) → M8 faces, #4 face: "render the spawn template through the CLI for a real `(workflow, task_id)`, extract the rendered command verbatim, and **execute it as a process**, asserting it resolves to the real `jigc workflow … --task` verb"). A CLI integration test renders the shipped spawn template via T1's `render_spawn` for a **real `(workflow, task_id)`** drawn from an actual milestone sub-task in a throwaway repo, **extracts the backticked `jigc workflow … --task <id>` command verbatim** from the rendered output (the emitted-bytes contract — never a hand-written `jigc workflow --task` in test code), and **executes that command as a process** (the built binary), asserting it **resolves to the real re-entry verb** (the M8-inc-2 `Command::Workflow` path — composes the sub-task / surfaces a genuine `workflow-refs.*` finding, never clap's "unrecognized subcommand" / unknown-verb error — guarding the L1 landmine that the template names a nonexistent command). **Done:** the test (a) provisions a real milestone sub-task (`jigc milestone create`/`add-task`), renders the template for that `(workflow, sub-task-id)`, parses the backticked span out of the rendered bytes, and runs it as a subprocess; (b) asserts the executed command is recognized as the `jigc workflow … --task` verb (reaches re-entry compose / a domain finding, not a clap parse error / nonexistent-command failure); gate green. Files: `crates/cli/tests/` (a new `spawn_template_executes.rs`). ([increment-workflow.md](implementation/increment-workflow.md) → M8 faces, #4 face; [roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md) increment-4 bullet 3.)

No new fork surfaced: the increment is fully covered by the milestone's settled decisions (2026-06-04 M8 planning entry — install-time launch-template validation = **full** with a *decidable* forbidden-pattern rule, single-line invocation, no `<<author:`/ATX-heading/`Run:`/`Spawn:`/`> ` markers, ≤1 newline, exactly one backticked span; the spawn binding = a launch template in the profile, CLI renders the fan-out dispatch through it; the #4 face — the adapter-rendered launch line is an emitted-bytes contract, render-and-execute it to guard the L1 landmine) and the locked [assistant-adapter.md](design/assistant-adapter.md) (Bind the spawn mechanism, The adapter profile). **All three *Grouped scope* bullets map to a task:** bullet 1 *`AdapterProfile` gains a `spawn` field (stop rejecting under `deny_unknown_fields`) + the launch-template render* → **T1**; bullet 2 *install-time schema validation — the full decidable rule, run at install, a broken template fails install* → **T2** (the decidable rule as a pure function with full clause coverage) + **T3** (wired into the install path, proven through the binary with a good template + several rejected ones); bullet 3 *the #4 face — render through the CLI, extract verbatim, execute as a process, assert it resolves to the real `jigc workflow … --task` verb* → **T4**. The `sub-task`/`milestone-execution` pack workflows + per-sub-task commits + `finalize.fan-out.squash` (increment 5) and the genuine two-half acceptance (increment 6) stay out — not narrowed here. **Scope-honesty note — the install *trigger* surface (tracked, not silently narrowed):** roadmap bullet 2 names "`setup`/`adapter install`/`regenerate`" as the trigger points, but the **only install-path command in MVP is `jigc setup`** — `jigc adapter install --assistant claude-code` is a `setup` synonym / future `--assistant` selector (the selector is marked post-MVP at `adapter.rs:34`/`setup.rs:36`) and "regenerate" is the upgrade-path regeneration ([assistant-adapter.md](design/assistant-adapter.md) line 20); **no `adapter install`/`adapter regenerate` verb exists and none of M8's prior increments added one**. So T3 wires validation into the single install path (`setup::install`) that exists — the rule fires at *every* install trigger present, and the *Deliverable* proves it "through the binary (setup …)". This is **not** a reduction of bullet 2 (building net-new `adapter install`/`regenerate` verbs is not in this increment's grounded surface, nor a roadmap deliverable for inc 4); it is recorded so the validator tracks the trigger-surface fact rather than treating a missing verb as a punted deliverable. **Verified-fact grounding the executor must treat as red-step assumptions, not trust blindly:** (a) `AdapterProfile` is `deny_unknown_fields` with **no `spawn` field today** — a `spawn:` block in the profile **fails to parse now**; T1's red step must prove the field newly *parses* (load a `spawn:`-carrying profile), never assume the model already tolerates it; (b) there is **no template-render helper** — `render_spawn` is net-new lexical substitution (`{{workflow}}`/`{{task_id}}` are plain template tokens, *not* the engine `{{…}}` data-value grammar — do not route them through `data_value`/compose); (c) there is **no install-time template validation today** — `setup::install` runs four steps, none touching a spawn template; T3 is net-new wiring, the red step must show a broken profile *failing install* where today it would install clean (the field doesn't even parse, so "today" means after T1); (d) the **`jigc workflow <W> --task <id>` verb exists** (M8 inc 2) — T4 executes the rendered command and asserts it reaches *that* verb's compose/domain-finding path, never a clap unknown-subcommand error; (e) the install trigger is **`jigc setup`**, *not* a separate `adapter install`/`regenerate` verb (the scope-honesty note above). Doc-elaboration pins the executor makes (not forks): (i) the `SpawnTarget`/`spawn` field shape + the `spawn()` accessor (mirroring `reference()`/`hook()`), golden-locked via the refreshed `claude-code.yaml` bytes snapshot; (ii) the `validate_spawn_template` return type (a typed reason enum naming the violated clause vs a `Result<(), String>` pointer message) and the `setup.*` check-id (`setup.spawn-template` or similar), located + routed; (iii) the backtick-span extraction in T4 (the lexical parse of the single backticked span the rule guarantees) and how the rendered command is executed (the built-binary subprocess idiom the existing CLI integration tests use); (iv) the test-file homes (extend `adapter.rs`/`setup.rs`/`tests/setup.rs` vs a new `tests/spawn_template_executes.rs`).

## 2026-06-05 — M8 Increment 4 T3 landed: spawn-template validation wired into the install path

T3 landed (2026-06-05): `setup::install` gains a **step 0** that runs T2's `validate_spawn_template` over the loaded profile's `spawn.template` **before any host-file write** — a violation returns a single blocking **`setup.spawn-template`** finding whose **route is the violated clause's `SpawnTemplateReason` pointer** (pin (ii) resolved: typed reason `Display` as the route, `setup.spawn-template` check-id), mapped to a non-zero exit by the existing `setup.*` dispatcher arm; the shipped (valid) profile passes, so a real `jigc setup` is unbroken, and a rejected template touches nothing on disk (the gate runs first). `setup.rs` unit tests assert both halves (shipped passes; a `{{task_id}}`-stripped template yields the blocking finding routed to the missing-placeholder clause + no `CLAUDE.md` written). **Binary-vs-unit split for the rejected half (sub-decision, not a fork):** the CLI integration test (`tests/setup.rs`) proves the *good* template through the real binary (clean install, no `setup.spawn-template` on stderr); the "several rejected templates each fail install" contract is proven at the `install()` function boundary the binary calls (the `setup.rs` unit test + T2's full clause table), because the binary loads **only the embedded (valid) profile** and the MVP ships **no adapter-profile-source override** — the `FilesystemPack` seam is pack-scoped, not adapter (`design/overrides.md`), and a net-new `JIGC_ADAPTERS_DIR`-style override is out of this increment's grounded surface. No new fork — within pin (ii)/(iv) latitude.

## 2026-06-05 — M8 Increment 5 build halt (Plan): the milestone-execution invocation surface — settled

The inc-5 build-planner **halted on a genuine fork** (correctly — it refused to silently pick): increment 5's headline command was specified two incompatible ways across **locked** docs. `worked-examples.md` flow 10 used `jigc start --workflow milestone-execution --task <milestone-id>` (`--workflow` + `--task` combined, `--task` naming a *milestone*), but `write-commands.md` + the M8-Settle reaffirmation + the built clap surface (`cli.rs` `conflicts_with`) make `start --workflow` **mutually exclusive with `--task`**, and `start --task` a *top-level-task resume* of the task's own recorded workflow — neither path composes a `creates-task: false` workflow over a milestone. **A planning miss:** flow 10 reused a `start` command shape without reconciling it against the exclusivity reaffirmed in the same planning pass; the design-review probed the sub-agent re-entry verb but not this top-level milestone invocation.

**Settled (human-decided): a new milestone-family verb `jigc milestone execute <milestone-id>`** — composes the `creates-task: false` `milestone-execution` workflow over the milestone, feeding the id-sorted task list into `{{milestone.tasks}}`, **minting nothing**. *Why this over the alternatives:* it fits the existing `jigc milestone` family (`create`/`add-task`/`add-from-spec`/`execute`/`join`/`finalize`), keeps `<id>` unambiguously a milestone, and **touches `start`'s settled `--workflow`/`--task` exclusivity not at all** — vs (A) relaxing that exclusivity (invasive; overloads `--task` to sometimes mean a milestone; contradicts "`start --task` stays top-level resume") or (B) overloading the sub-agent re-entry verb `jigc workflow <W> --task <milestone-id>` (a milestone is not a sub-task; would special-case the W-equality guard and lie with `--task`). Docs reconciled so the two locked docs agree: `worked-examples.md` flow 10 → `jigc milestone execute cache-hardening`; `write-commands.md` → the `execute` verb added to the milestone family + an explicit "not on `jigc start`" note; `roadmap.md` inc-5 → the `jigc milestone execute` verb + the `ComposeContext.milestone`-feeding dispatch added as inc-5's **first** grouped-scope bullet (cut ahead of the pack workflows + squash knob — every production `ComposeContext` passes `milestone: Vec::new()` today, so this is the net-new wiring that makes `{{milestone.tasks}}` resolve against a real milestone). Which workflow `execute` composes is the pack's single `milestone-execution` today; a cascade knob can choose among several when a second earns it (the `default-workflow`/router evolution). Increments 1–4 stand (committed, clean); the build resumes at inc-5 Plan against the reconciled docs.

## 2026-06-05 — M8 Increment 5 planning: decomposition

Increment 5 — the `sub-task` + `milestone-execution` pack workflows + per-sub-task commits + `finalize.fan-out.squash` — cut into **4 ordered, single-concern tasks** ([roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md#increment-5--the-sub-task--milestone-execution-pack-workflows--per-sub-task-commits--finalizefan-outsquash); design [workflow-dialect.md](design/workflow-dialect.md) → On-disk definition format, [finalize.md](design/finalize.md#fan-out-finalize), [write-commands.md](design/write-commands.md) → Minting a milestone; the invocation surface settled this date, above). Serial spine, faithful to the roadmap's "verb cut first": the `jigc milestone execute` verb + `ComposeContext.milestone`-feeding dispatch (T1, the net-new compose wiring), then the fanned `sub-task` pack workflow + its steps (T2, the fan-out target), then the `milestone-execution` workflow + its fan-out/join/finalize steps proven end-to-end through the binary (T3, where T1's verb finally composes the real workflow and emits N id-ordered `Spawn:` directives), then the `finalize.fan-out.squash` knob + both commit modes (T4). *Why this order:* T1 ships the verb but composes only a fixture workflow (the real `milestone-execution` is T3) — the milestone-feeding contract (`{{milestone.tasks}}` resolves the id-sorted list) is proven at the dispatch boundary in T1; T2 ships the `run:` target the `milestone-execution` fan-out references, so T3's `fan-out-join-paired` + spawn emission compose green only once `sub-task` exists; T4's squash modes ride on the milestone's committed sub-task areas + their authored commit docs, which the `sub-task` workflow (T2) is what produces.

**Ground-truth that shaped the cut (verified against the code, not assumed):** (a) the compose-side fan-out spine is **already built** (inc-1/2/3) — `StepKind::FanOut { over, run }`/`Join` parse + honor step front-matter (`compose.rs:926`), `emit_fan_out_spawns` emits one `` Spawn: `jigc workflow <run> --task <id>` `` per id-sorted id (`compose.rs:743`), the `{{milestone.tasks}}` data-value root resolves `ComposeContext.milestone` to the id-sorted collection (`data_value.rs:412`), and the `spawn-marker-not-shadowed` + `fan-out-join-paired` `workflow-refs` checks are floored. So inc-5 builds **no new compose/emit code** — it ships the *pack files* that drive it + the *verb* that feeds a real milestone. (b) `ComposeContext.milestone` exists but **every production `ComposeContext` passes `milestone: Vec::new()`** (`start.rs:391`, `start.rs:1146`) — T1's dispatch is the lone net-new wiring that feeds it the real `TaskList::enumerate()` output. (c) the `sub-task` workflow declares `creates-task: true`, and `selectable_workflows` (`start.rs:749`) enumerates **every** `creates-task: true` pack workflow into the router `{{catalog}}` (the design rule, `workflow-dialect.md` line 246: "selectable work-workflows = those declaring `creates-task: true`") — so shipping `sub-task.yaml` makes it surface in the router catalog and **breaks `routing_loop.rs::step_5_the_live_router_catalog_lists_exactly_the_four_real_work_workflows`** (an exhaustive `== four` assertion, `routing_loop.rs:309`); `selectable_workflows` also **errors if a `creates-task: true` workflow has no `when` hint** (`start.rs:757`), which is why the roadmap names "a `when` hint" in scope. (d) `squash` **does not exist anywhere** (`rg squash` over `crates` = empty) — `jigc milestone finalize` today unconditionally synthesizes the single aggregate commit (the `squash: true` form, `milestone.rs:409` `synthesized_message`); the `false` per-sub-task-commit mode is wholly net-new. (e) the `milestone-finalize` step's `Run:` command needs `jigc milestone finalize <id>`, but `milestone-execution` is `creates-task: false` so a `{from: task.*}` arg is the `task-ref-in-no-task-workflow` conformance error and **no `milestone.id` data-value root exists** — the resolvable shape is an `{agent: milestone_id, hint: …}` command-ref arg (the `create-adr` `{agent:}` precedent, `commands.yaml`), needing no new root.

- **T1 — the `jigc milestone execute <id>` verb + the `ComposeContext.milestone`-feeding dispatch (the compose path, cut first).** Add an `Execute { milestone_id }` arm to `MilestoneCommand` (`milestone.rs`, clap + dispatch): resolve the milestone work-unit (unknown id → routed block before any compose, the `dispatch_finalize` precedent), read its id-sorted `TaskList::enumerate()`, build a `creates-task: false` `ComposeContext` with `task: None` + `milestone: <the id-sorted ids>` (the lone production site that feeds `milestone` non-empty), and compose the named workflow over it — printing the composed view (no mint, no working area). Because the real `milestone-execution` pack workflow is T3, T1 proves the **milestone-feeding contract** at the dispatch boundary against a composable `creates-task: false` fixture workflow carrying a `fan-out` over `{{milestone.tasks}}` (the `FixturePack`/injected-workflow idiom `start.rs` compose tests already use). **Done:** a CLI/`milestone.rs` test asserts (a) `jigc milestone execute <unknown>` routes a blocking unknown-milestone finding + non-zero exit, composing nothing; (b) executing over a milestone with ≥2 sub-tasks builds a `ComposeContext` whose `milestone` field equals the id-sorted `TaskList::enumerate()` output, and a fan-out fixture workflow composed through that context emits one `` Spawn: `jigc workflow <run> --task <id>` `` per id **in id-sorted order** (the feeding reaches `{{milestone.tasks}}`); gate green. Files: `crates/cli/src/milestone.rs`, `crates/cli/src/cli.rs`, `crates/cli/src/render.rs` (compose-view render, if needed), `crates/cli/tests/milestone.rs`. ([roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md) inc-5 bullet 1; [write-commands.md](design/write-commands.md) → Minting a milestone; [DECISIONS.md](DECISIONS.md) 2026-06-05 — the invocation-surface settlement.)
- **T2 — the `sub-task` pack workflow + its `locate`/`implement`/`author-commit` steps (the fanned target).** Ship `pack/workflows/sub-task.yaml` (`creates-task: true`, `allows-create: [{type: adr, as: decision}]`, a `when` hint, **no `finalize` step** — fan-out-free by construction, the no-nested-fan-out structural guarantee) with body `{{ include: step:locate }}` / `{{ include: step:implement }}` / `{{ include: step:author-commit }}`. `locate` + `implement` already ship and are reused unchanged; **`author-commit` is the one net-new step** — the sub-agent authors its own `commit` doc prose (`{{ cli.set-commit-summary }}` + `<<author: {{ task.commit#summary }}>>`) but **never finalizes** (no `finalize-task` ref — the parent's `jigc milestone finalize` is the only commit boundary). **Handle the router-catalog interaction (verified-fact (c), not a silent reduction):** `sub-task` being `creates-task: true` surfaces it in the router `{{catalog}}` per the settled design rule, so **update `routing_loop.rs` step 5's exhaustive `== four` assertion to expect `sub-task` (+ its `when` hint)** — the catalog genuinely grows by one; do not suppress it (that would special-case the catalog rule). **Done:** `jigc start --workflow sub-task "<intent>"` composes green through the binary (mints a task, emits the `locate`/`implement`/`author-commit` bodies, **no `Run: jigc task finalize`** line present); a compose/conformance test asserts `sub-task` carries no `join`/`fan-out`/`finalize` step (fan-out-free); the updated `routing_loop.rs` step 5 passes with `sub-task` in the catalog; gate green. Files: `crates/cli/pack/workflows/sub-task.yaml`, `crates/cli/pack/steps/author-commit.yaml`, `crates/cli/tests/routing_loop.rs`, `crates/cli/tests/start_compose.rs` (or `milestone.rs`). ([roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md) inc-5 bullet 2; [workflow-dialect.md](design/workflow-dialect.md) → On-disk definition format — the fan-out-free `sub-task`.)
- **T3 — the `milestone-execution` pack workflow + its `implement-tasks`/`join-tasks`/`milestone-finalize` steps, proven end-to-end through the binary.** Ship `pack/workflows/milestone-execution.yaml` (`creates-task: false`, a `when` hint) with body `{{ include: step:implement-tasks }}` / `{{ include: step:join-tasks }}` / `{{ include: step:milestone-finalize }}`, and its three net-new steps: `implement-tasks` (a `fan-out` step — `fan-out:\n  over: "{{ milestone.tasks }}"\n  run:  workflow:sub-task`), `join-tasks` (a `join: {}` barrier), `milestone-finalize` (a plain step whose `Run:` resolves a **new `milestone-finalize` command-ref** — `jigc milestone finalize {agent: milestone_id}`, added to `commands.yaml`; the `{agent:}` shape avoids the absent `milestone.id` root per verified-fact (e)). This is where T1's `jigc milestone execute` first composes the **real** workflow. **Done:** through the binary, `jigc milestone execute <milestone-id>` over a fixture milestone with ≥2 sub-tasks emits exactly N `` Spawn: `jigc workflow sub-task --task <id>` `` directives **in id-sorted order**, followed by the `join` barrier prose and the resolved `` Run: `jigc milestone finalize <id>` `` line; the composition satisfies `fan-out-join-paired` (composes green, no `workflow-refs` block); a determinism assertion composes the same milestone under ≥2 divergent enumeration/read orders and asserts byte-identical `Spawn:` output (hardening #7); gate green. Files: `crates/cli/pack/workflows/milestone-execution.yaml`, `crates/cli/pack/steps/implement-tasks.yaml`, `crates/cli/pack/steps/join-tasks.yaml`, `crates/cli/pack/steps/milestone-finalize.yaml`, `crates/cli/pack/config/commands.yaml`, `crates/cli/tests/milestone.rs`. ([roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md) inc-5 bullet 2; [workflow-dialect.md](design/workflow-dialect.md) → On-disk definition format — the `milestone-execution` fan-out/join composition.)
- **T4 — the `finalize.fan-out.squash` knob + both commit modes (default `true` = the M7 aggregate; `false` = per-sub-task authored commits in id order, plus the parent's).** Declare `finalize.fan-out.squash` in `knobs.yaml` (a `bool`, default `true`). In `jigc milestone finalize`, read the resolved knob: `true` keeps the existing single CLI-`synthesized_message` aggregate commit unchanged (the read-side determinism guard — no-knob/default path byte-identical to today); `false` renders **one commit per sub-task in id-sorted order** (each sub-area's authored `commit:<sub-id>` doc rendered via the existing `render_commit_message`, sub-agents authored the prose, the CLI owns ordering + git), **plus the parent's** synthesized aggregate — the commit *sequence* deterministic (byte-identical across feed orders) even though each carries LLM prose. **Done:** a `knobs.yaml`/cascade test asserts `finalize.fan-out.squash` resolves `true` by default and is settable to `false`; a milestone-finalize test (engine + a CLI integration test through the binary) asserts (a) default `squash: true` lands **one** commit with the synthesized message (unchanged from inc-4); (b) `squash: false` over a milestone whose ≥2 sub-tasks each authored a `commit` doc lands **N+1 commits** — the N sub-task commits in **id-sorted order** rendering each authored message, then the parent's — and that the committed commit *sequence* (messages + tree) is **byte-identical across ≥2 divergent feed orders** (hardening #7); gate green. Files: `crates/cli/pack/config/knobs.yaml`, `crates/cli/src/milestone.rs`, `crates/engine/src/finalize.rs` (or `milestone.rs` — the per-sub-task render+commit sequence), `crates/cli/tests/milestone.rs`. ([roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md) inc-5 bullet 3; [finalize.md](design/finalize.md#fan-out-finalize) → the two squash modes.)

## 2026-06-05 — M8 Increment 5 T1 landed: `jigc milestone execute <id>` + the `ComposeContext.milestone`-feeding dispatch

T1 landed: `MilestoneCommand::Execute { milestone_id }` (clap + its own `dispatch_execute` arm, the `dispatch_join`/`dispatch_finalize` precedent — it emits a composed view, not a one-line summary). `run_execute` resolves the milestone dir (unknown → routed block **before any compose**, `commits/composes nothing`), reads `read_task_list(dir).enumerate()` (id-sorted), and calls the net-new `start::execute_milestone_in_repo(repo_root, "milestone-execution", &ids)` — the **lone production site feeding `ComposeContext.milestone` non-empty**. **Feeding implementation (not a fork):** `compose_core`/`compose_drained` gained a `milestone_ids: &[String]` parameter threaded into the no-task arm's `milestone:` field; every other caller passes `&[]` (the `creates-task: true` `build_context` arm keeps `Vec::new()`, and `start`'s router compose passes `&[]`) — so the no-milestone paths stay byte-identical. The pack workflow `milestone-execution` is T3, so T1 proves the feeding contract against a `FixturePack` `creates-task: false` fan-out fixture via the `#[cfg(test)]` seam `start::execute_milestone_core` (a `pub(crate)` test affordance that builds an empty `ComposeOverrides` and calls `compose_core`; `PackStepSource` made `pub(crate)` for the same). **No `render.rs` change** — the composed view renders through the existing `render::composed`. Tests: a `milestone.rs` unit test feeds the id-sorted list in NON-id order and asserts one `` Spawn: `jigc workflow sub-task --task <id>` `` per id **id-sorted on the emitted bytes**, plus byte-identical output under the **reversed** feed (hardening #7 — the resolver sorts on resolve); a `tests/milestone.rs` integration test drives the binary for the unknown-milestone block (non-zero, routed, empty stdout). Gate green. No new fork.

## 2026-06-05 — M8 Increment 5 T3 landed: the `milestone-execution` pack workflow + its `implement-tasks`/`join-tasks`/`milestone-finalize` steps

T3 landed: the pack now ships the `creates-task: false` **`milestone-execution`** workflow (`workflows/milestone-execution.yaml`, a `when` hint + the three ordered includes `implement-tasks` → `join-tasks` → `milestone-finalize`) and its three steps. `steps/implement-tasks.yaml` is the **fan-out** (`fan-out: { over: "{{ milestone.tasks }}", run: workflow:sub-task }`); `steps/join-tasks.yaml` is the **join** barrier (`join: {}`, prose naming "merged by task-id order"); `steps/milestone-finalize.yaml` is a plain step whose body resolves a NEW `milestone-finalize` command-ref. The command-ref (`config/commands.yaml`) uses the **`{agent: milestone_id}`** arg shape — verified-fact (e): NOT a `{from: task.*}` path (a `task-ref-in-no-task-workflow` block in a `creates-task: false` workflow) and NOT a `milestone.id` data-value root (doesn't exist) — so it renders `` Run: `jigc milestone finalize <MILESTONE_ID>` `` (the agent fills the bare uppercase marker at run-time, like `create-adr`'s `<TITLE>`). This is where T1's `jigc milestone execute <id>` verb **first composes the real pack workflow** end-to-end: no new `emit_fan_out_spawns`/`StepKind`/`workflow-refs` code (verified-fact (a) held). Tests (`tests/milestone.rs`): `milestone_execute_composes_the_real_fanout_join_finalize_workflow` drives the binary over a real 2-sub-task milestone and asserts — off the **composed bytes the agent runs**, never a reconstructed equivalent — exactly N id-sorted `` Spawn: `jigc workflow sub-task --task <id>` `` directives (added zebra-then-alpha, emitted alpha-before-zebra), the join barrier prose **between** the spawns and the finalize line, and the resolved `Run:` line, with no `fan-out-join-paired` block (the pairing is satisfied); `milestone_execute_spawn_emit_is_byte_identical_across_divergent_add_orders` (hardening #7) mints the same three sub-tasks under id-order **and its reverse** in two repos and asserts a byte-identical `Spawn:` block (the enumeration sorts on resolve). The `pack.rs` `embedded_pack_lists_the_mvp_steps` enumeration grew the three new step ids (in sorted position). Gate green. No new fork.

No new fork surfaced beyond the **already-settled** invocation surface (this date, above — `jigc milestone execute <id>`, the human-decided resolution the build is resuming against; this plan does **not** re-litigate it). The increment is otherwise fully covered by the milestone's settled decisions (2026-06-04 M8 planning entry + the `fan-out` finalize squash-knob design; the `sub-task`/`milestone-execution` workflow shapes in [workflow-dialect.md](design/workflow-dialect.md)) and the locked [finalize.md](design/finalize.md) / [workflow-dialect.md](design/workflow-dialect.md) / [write-commands.md](design/write-commands.md). **All three *Grouped scope* bullets map to a task:** bullet 1 *the `jigc milestone execute` verb + `ComposeContext.milestone`-feeding dispatch* → **T1**; bullet 2 *the `sub-task` workflow + its `locate`/`implement`/`author-commit` steps* → **T2**, and *the `milestone-execution` workflow + its `implement-tasks`/`join-tasks`/`milestone-finalize` steps + the `when` hint* → **T3** (bullet 2 is deliberately staged across T2→T3: T2 ships the fanned `run:` target the T3 fan-out references, so neither half is left to "a later task" in the abstract — each names the other); bullet 3 *the `finalize.fan-out.squash` knob + both modes + the `knobs.yaml` declaration* → **T4**. Increment 6 (the two-half flow-10 acceptance — the N-process binary sim + the genuine concurrent Claude-Code spawn artifact) stays out, not narrowed here. **Verified-fact grounding the executor must treat as red-step assumptions, not trust blindly:** (i) the compose/emit spine is built — T1/T3 ship pack files + the verb, **no new `emit_fan_out_spawns`/`StepKind`/`workflow-refs` code** (verified-fact (a)); a red step must show the *real* `milestone-execution` pack workflow emitting N `Spawn:` lines, never re-test the engine emitter in isolation; (ii) shipping `sub-task.yaml` **grows the router catalog** and breaks the exhaustive `routing_loop.rs` step-5 assertion (verified-fact (c)) — T2 must update that test to expect `sub-task`, never suppress the catalog entry; (iii) `squash` is **wholly net-new** (verified-fact (d)) — T4's red step must show `squash: false` landing N+1 commits where today only the single aggregate lands, and the **default-path bytes unchanged**; (iv) the `milestone-finalize` step's command-ref uses `{agent: milestone_id}` (verified-fact (e)), *not* a `{from: task.*}`/`milestone.id` path (the former is a `task-ref-in-no-task-workflow` block, the latter doesn't exist). Doc-elaboration pins the executor makes (not forks): the `sub-task`/`milestone-execution` `when` hint wording (any non-empty selection hint satisfies `selectable_workflows`); the `author-commit` step body shape (mirror `implement`'s commit-summary authoring, minus finalize); the per-sub-task commit-render-and-stage sequence home in T4 (a new `finalize.rs`/`milestone.rs` fn vs extending `execute_finalize_plan`), golden-locked where the committed message bytes matter.

T4 landed (2026-06-05): the **`finalize.fan-out.squash`** knob + both commit modes. `config/knobs.yaml` declares it as a `bool`, default `"true"`, **no floor** (tunable) — `loaded_knobs_seed_the_pack_default_scalar_surface` + `pack.rs`'s embedded-knobs snapshot grew the key in sorted position; `squash_knob_defaults_true_and_is_settable_to_false` asserts it resolves `true` by default and `false` via a project `scalar:` set (adjudicated by the same `check_value` — a bool accepts `true`/`false`). New engine planner `finalize::render_subtask_messages(sub_ids, tasks_root, commit_schema) -> Result<Vec<String>, Vec<Finding>>` renders each sub-task's authored `commit:<sub-id>` doc (`<tasks_root>/<sub-id>/docs/commit:<sub-id>.md`) via the shared `render_commit_message`, in the **given (id-sorted) order** — pure, no git; a missing authored commit doc is the blocking `finalize.render-io` finding (the `plan_finalize` render precedent). `crate::milestone::run_milestone_finalize` reads the knob via `start::resolve_severity_cascade` (a missing `.jigc/config/` layer resolves to the pack-default base → `true`, so the default path is byte-identical to M7's single synthesized aggregate — the read-side determinism guard); on `false` it lays down one `--allow-empty` commit per sub-task (new `task::commit_empty_message`, never `--no-verify`) in `TaskList::enumerate()` id-order, **then** the parent's synthesized aggregate. Doc-elaboration pin (not a fork — the planning-cut grants "the per-sub-task commit-render-and-stage sequence home in T4"): the per-sub-task commits are **tree-empty** (`--allow-empty`) carrying only the authored prose; the **merged tree lands once in the parent aggregate** (identical to `squash:true`), avoiding fragile suffix-resolved-doc→origin-area attribution while keeping each sub-task a real commit in the deterministic sequence. Ordering pin: the sub-task commits are laid down **after** the planner's preflight validates `base == HEAD` (against the pre-boundary HEAD) and **before** `execute_finalize_plan` — the boundary's base invariant is the planner's single call; the empty commits then advance HEAD and the parent aggregate re-checks nothing. Tests (`tests/milestone.rs`): `milestone_finalize_squash_false_lands_n_plus_one_commits_in_id_order` (N+1=3 commits, the two sub-task subjects id-sorted then the parent aggregate, the merged ADRs committed) + `milestone_finalize_squash_false_sequence_is_byte_identical_across_feed_orders` (hardening #7 — same two sub-tasks finalized under id-order **and its reverse** land a byte-identical message sequence + tree). The M7 `squash:true` one-commit test is unchanged (default-path bytes preserved). Incidental: T3 (`eaf43ab`) shipped the `milestone-finalize` `commands.yaml` entry but left `compose.rs`'s `command_catalog_loads_three_arg_kinds` inline snapshot stale (gate red on `main`); accepted the snapshot (purely T3's already-committed catalog entry). Gate green. No new fork.

## 2026-06-05 — M8 Increment 6 planning: decomposition

Increment 6 — **the two-half acceptance (flow 10, the headline)** — cut into **4 ordered, single-concern tasks** ([roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md#increment-6--the-two-half-acceptance-flow-10-the-headline); acceptance = [worked-examples.md](design/worked-examples.md) → flow 10; the spawn-class audit artifact = [milestone-completion-workflow.md](implementation/milestone-completion-workflow.md) → step 1). This is an **acceptance-only increment** — the full M8 control-plane arc (compose-side fan-out spine, `jigc workflow --task` re-entry, the write-time `--task` barrier + copy-on-first-touch, the adapter spawn binding + install validation, the `sub-task`/`milestone-execution` pack workflows + `finalize.fan-out.squash`) is **built and wired through the binary** by increments 1–5; increment 6 ships **no new product code**, only the two acceptance artifacts the deliverable names: Half A (an automated `cargo test` e2e) and the Half-B *procedure* (documented here, *run* by the orchestrator at completion). Serial spine, faithful to flow 10's own three-step structure: the deterministic-emit + L1-guard gate first (T1), the real write→join seam next (T2), the byte-identical-finalize-across-orders headline (T3), the Half-B runnable procedure last (T4).

**Ground-truth that shaped the cut (verified against the running binary + the committed tests, not assumed):** (a) the **whole flow-10 arc runs end-to-end through the built binary today** — driving `jigc milestone create cache-rework` → `add-task … --workflow sub-task` ×2 → `jigc milestone execute cache-rework` emits, **id-sorted**, one `` Spawn: `jigc workflow sub-task --task <id>` `` per sub-task + the join-barrier prose + a resolved `` Run: `jigc milestone finalize <id>` `` line; then `jigc workflow sub-task --task <id>` provisions the write-ready area (the `author-commit` step's `Run: jigc doc set-slot commit:<id>#summary` + its `<<author:>>` slot) and `jigc doc create adr --title … --task <id>` + `set-slot adr:<slug>#decision --task <id>` write a `created` ADR into the isolated area, and `jigc milestone finalize cache-rework` lands **one aggregate commit** promoting both ADRs into `decisions/` (squash default `true`). So every piece Half A drives is real, not a stub. (b) The Half-A test idioms are **already committed and reusable**: `flow9_seam.rs` runs `jigc doc … --task` writes through the binary into milestone sub-areas (the seam helpers `milestone_with_two_subtasks`/`run_doc`/`docs_area`); `spawn_template_executes.rs` lifts the **shipped** `adapters/claude-code.yaml` template verbatim, renders it via the two-token `{{workflow}}`/`{{task_id}}` substitution, extracts the single backticked span, and **executes it as a process** asserting it reaches the real `jigc workflow … --task` verb (the #4-face L1 guard); `flow9_milestone_join.rs` asserts **byte-identical commit MESSAGE + committed TREE hash** across **three divergent feed orders** (id / reverse / seed-shuffled) via `head_message`/`head_tree`-style helpers. T1–T3 compose these idioms over the *real-write* flow-10 arc — they do **not** invent a new test harness. (c) `jigc milestone execute <id>` is the **settled invocation surface** (2026-06-05, above — the inc-5 fork resolution); flow 10 in `worked-examples.md` already uses `jigc milestone execute cache-hardening`, so the e2e drives the reconciled command, not the retired `start --workflow … --task` shape. (d) The Half-B section of `worked-examples.md` flow 10 (lines ~630–634) is **conceptual** (one paragraph: the orchestrator drives a genuine Task-tool spawn, asserts the committed tree-hash matches Half A's golden, witnesses the blackboard invariant); the increment's *Grouped scope* bullet 2 requires the procedure be **documented** runnably (the exact fixture reused from Half A, the precise tree-hash assertion, the blackboard-witness checklist, the hollow-spawn / missing-artifact = blocking-completion-finding gate) — T4 expands that section from conceptual to a runnable procedure, cross-referencing (never restating) the generic spawn-class artifact spec in `milestone-completion-workflow.md`.

- **T1 — Half-A step 1: deterministic `Spawn:` emit + the L1 launch-line guard (a `cargo test`).** A new e2e `tests/flow10_acceptance.rs` (the `flow9_seam.rs`/`spawn_template_executes.rs` throwaway-repo + `CARGO_BIN_EXE_jigc` idiom): stand up a real milestone (`jigc milestone create` + `add-task --workflow sub-task` ×N, the titles deliberately **not** in id order so a sort is exercised), run `jigc milestone execute <id>` through the binary, and assert it emits **exactly N** `` Spawn: `jigc workflow sub-task --task <id>` `` directives **in id-sorted order** — asserted on the **emitted bytes the agent would run**, never a reconstruction (hardening #4); compose twice (or under a divergent add order) and assert the `Spawn:` block is **byte-identical** (hardening #7). Then the **L1 guard**: lift the **shipped** `adapters/claude-code.yaml` spawn template verbatim, render it for a real `(sub-task, <id>)`, extract the single backticked span, and **execute it as a process**, asserting it reaches the real `jigc workflow … --task` verb (composes, exit 0 — not a clap unknown-subcommand error), reusing `spawn_template_executes.rs`'s `shipped_spawn_template`/`render_spawn`/`backticked_span` pattern. **Done:** `cargo test --test flow10_acceptance` runs and the two assertions above pass through the real binary (N id-sorted Spawn lines, byte-identical across orders; the rendered launch line executes to the real re-entry verb); gate green. Files: `crates/cli/tests/flow10_acceptance.rs`. ([roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md) inc-6 bullet 1; [worked-examples.md](design/worked-examples.md) → flow 10 Half-A steps 1; [increment-workflow.md](implementation/increment-workflow.md) → M8 faces #4, hardening #7.)
- **T2 — Half-A step 2: the real write→join seam (N separate binary processes, no hand-staging).** Extend `tests/flow10_acceptance.rs`: over the milestone from T1's shape, invoke `jigc workflow sub-task --task <id>` as **N separate binary processes** (one per sub-task — exactly what a real fanned sub-agent runs) to provision each write-ready area, then write into each isolated area through the real `--task` verbs (`jigc doc create adr --title … --task <id>` + `set-slot adr:<slug>#decision --from-file - --task <id>`, and at least one `edited-from-base` via a first `set-slot --task` against a base-committed ADR — copy-on-first-touch), producing exactly the `docs/<addr>.md` + conforming `provenance.json` the M7 join consumes (assert the manifest records `created` / `edited-from-base` as written — the `flow9_seam.rs` provenance assertions). Then `jigc milestone join <id>` consumes those **real-written** inputs and reports a clean merge (exit 0, both addresses in the overlay) — replacing flow-9's `stage_doc` hand-staging. **Done:** `cargo test --test flow10_acceptance` passes the seam assertions: N `jigc workflow … --task` processes + real `jigc doc … --task` writes produce the join's input artifacts (provenance verified), and `jigc milestone join` merges them clean through the binary; gate green. Files: `crates/cli/tests/flow10_acceptance.rs`. ([roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md) inc-6 bullet 1; [worked-examples.md](design/worked-examples.md) → flow 10 Half-A step 2; [increment-workflow.md](implementation/increment-workflow.md) → hardening #4 "drive the emitted bytes verbatim".)
- **T3 — Half-A step 3: byte-identical finalize across ≥2 divergent orders (the headline assertion).** Extend `tests/flow10_acceptance.rs`: feed the **identical** real-binary-produced sub-task area set (built via the T2 N-process write path) through the full `create`/`add-from-spec` → `jigc milestone execute` → `jigc milestone finalize` arc in **≥2 independent throwaway repos whose feed order is deliberately divergent** — canonical **id order** and its **reverse** (reverse mandatory — an id-ordered fixture makes completion order trivially equal id order and hides the bug; a third seed-shuffled repo welcome, the flow-9 idiom) — and assert the committed **commit MESSAGE *and* committed TREE hash are byte-identical across all orders** (the `flow9_milestone_join.rs` `head_message`/`head_tree` assertion, now over real-write inputs). Exercise the `add-from-spec`-seeded path within the arc (flow 10's documented `add-from-spec` entry) so the seeded-sub-task source is covered. **This produces the golden tree-hash Half-B (T4) must match.** **Done:** `cargo test --test flow10_acceptance` passes: the identical real-write area set finalized under id-order and reverse-order yields byte-identical committed message + tree hash; the `add-from-spec` arc is exercised; gate green. Files: `crates/cli/tests/flow10_acceptance.rs`. ([roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md) inc-6 bullet 1; [worked-examples.md](design/worked-examples.md) → flow 10 Half-A step 3; [increment-workflow.md](implementation/increment-workflow.md) → A second principle (M7) / hardening #7.)
- **T4 — Half-B: the runnable genuine-spawn procedure (documented, the orchestrator runs it at completion).** Expand `worked-examples.md` flow 10's Half-B section from conceptual to a **runnable procedure**: the orchestrator (the **main session** — a headless subagent cannot spawn the Task tool) drives a genuine concurrent Claude-Code-Task-tool spawn of ≥2 sub-agents over **the same fixture milestone Half A's golden uses**, each sub-agent re-entering via `jigc workflow sub-task --task <id>` into its isolated area and writing only through `jigc` (the write-time barrier refuses anything else); the join recombines and `jigc milestone finalize` commits; assert the committed **tree-hash matches Half A's T3 golden byte-for-byte**, and record the transcript **witnessing the blackboard invariant** (each sub-agent reached state via `jigc` only, no direct read of a sibling's area — a property no permutation test can see). State the gate explicitly: **the milestone is not shippable until this artifact exists and matches** — a missing artifact is a **blocking completion finding** (the hollow-spawn trap: never pass off Half A's N-process sim as the genuine-spawn proof). Cross-reference (never restate) the generic spawn-class artifact spec in [milestone-completion-workflow.md](implementation/milestone-completion-workflow.md) → step 1. **Done:** `worked-examples.md` flow 10 Half-B reads as a procedure an orchestrator can follow end-to-end (the exact fixture reused from Half A, the tree-hash match assertion, the blackboard-witness checklist, the not-shippable-until gate); no product code changes; gate green (docs-only; `fmt`/`clippy`/`test`/`build` unaffected). Files: `design/worked-examples.md`. ([roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md) inc-6 bullet 2; [worked-examples.md](design/worked-examples.md) → flow 10 Half-B; [milestone-completion-workflow.md](implementation/milestone-completion-workflow.md) → step 1 the spawn-class artifact; [increment-workflow.md](implementation/increment-workflow.md) → M8 faces "the capability gap".)

No new fork surfaced. The increment is fully covered by the milestone's settled decisions (the 2026-06-04 M8 planning entry + the M8 faces; the 2026-06-05 `jigc milestone execute` invocation surface) and the locked [worked-examples.md](design/worked-examples.md) flow 10 / [milestone-completion-workflow.md](implementation/milestone-completion-workflow.md) spawn-class-artifact spec. **Both *Grouped scope* bullets map to a task:** bullet 1 *the flow-10 Half-A e2e in a throwaway repo — the N-process sim through the real binary over the full `create`/`add-from-spec` → `milestone-execution` → join → finalize arc* → **T1+T2+T3** (deliberately staged across the three tasks along flow 10's own three Half-A steps: T1 the deterministic emit + L1 guard, T2 the real write→join seam, T3 the byte-identical finalize across orders — each names the others, so no half is left to "a later task" in the abstract); bullet 2 *the Half-B procedure: documented + run by the orchestrator at milestone completion* → **T4 documents it**; the *run* half is the orchestrator's milestone-completion audit phase, **not a build task** (a headless executor cannot spawn the Task tool — [increment-workflow.md](implementation/increment-workflow.md) → M8 faces "the capability gap"), so T4 delivers the in-scope, build-deliverable half (the runnable procedure) and the milestone-completion workflow owns the run. **Verified-fact the executor must treat as red-step assumptions, not trust blindly:** (i) the full flow-10 arc runs through the built binary today (verified by running it — ground-truth (a)); a red step must **drive the real binary** over the arc, never assert against a reconstruction or a stub. (ii) Half A ships **no new product code** — if any T1–T3 assertion can only pass by changing `crates/*/src`, that is a regression-find or a missed earlier-increment gap → **halt** to the orchestrator, do not silently add product code under an acceptance task. (iii) The shipped `adapters/claude-code.yaml` spawn template is the L1-guard contract (verified — `spawn_template_executes.rs`); render+execute the **shipped bytes**, never a hand-written launch line. Doc-elaboration pins the executor makes (not forks): the concrete fixture milestone slug/titles + N for `flow10_acceptance.rs` (mirror `flow9_seam.rs`'s `cache-rework` shape); whether T1–T3 are three `#[test]` fns in one file or share helpers (single-concern is the *assertion set*, not the file count); the exact Half-B procedure wording in `worked-examples.md` (within the locked flow-10 framing).

## 2026-06-05 — M8 completion remediation: the `JIGC_ADAPTERS_DIR` adapter-source seam

Audit caveat (e2e, not a regression): the install-time spawn-template gate (`validate_spawn_template`, wired into `setup::install` step 0) had its **rejection** path unreachable through the shipped binary — the binary embeds only the valid `claude-code.yaml`, with no adapter-profile-source override, so only the ACCEPT path was binary-observable; the decidable rejection rule was unit-test-only. **Remedy:** add a **`JIGC_ADAPTERS_DIR`** env override — the adapter analogue of [`crate::pack`]'s `JIGC_PACK_DIR` ([`design/overrides.md`] → the `FilesystemPack` seam; 2026-06-03 — `make_pack()` factory). When set, `adapter::load_profile` reads the profile YAML live from `<dir>/<assistant>.yaml`; unset, the binary-embedded profile is used exactly as before. The load routes through **one seam** (`load_profile` → `load_profile_from(assistant, env)`, the parallel-safe testable core), so the obligation is "every production profile load honors the env," not a site count (the M5 N1 lesson). **Determinism guard:** with the env unset the seam is inert — `jigc setup` output stays byte-identical to today (proven in `tests/adapter_source_setup.rs`). The seam exists to make the gate binary-observable: a deliberately-broken spawn template driven through the real `jigc setup` now rejects with the blocking `setup.spawn-template` finding routed to the violated clause, non-zero exit, **no host file written** (the gate runs before any write); ≥2 distinct violation kinds are exercised through the binary. Scope: the seam + its tests only; the validation rule itself is unchanged (correct, unit-tested). Files: `crates/cli/src/adapter.rs`, `crates/cli/tests/adapter_source_setup.rs`, `crates/cli/tests/setup.rs` (stale "no adapter override" comment retargeted at the new seam).

## 2026-06-05 — M8 (milestone execution — control plane) shipped

M8's control plane is built end-to-end and the milestone-execution primitive is **complete** (M7 data plane + M8 control plane). The build ran via the [milestone-build](implementation/milestone-build) harness across six risk-first increments with **one halt** (inc-5 Plan — the milestone-execution invocation-surface fork, human-settled to `jigc milestone execute <id>`; 2026-06-05 above) and **0 in-increment fix rounds** otherwise. Shipped: the `{{milestone.tasks}}` root, `fan-out`/`join` step kinds (parse-and-honor), the `Spawn:` 5th emit class + `workflow-refs.{spawn-marker-not-shadowed,fan-out-join-paired}` (inventory 18→20 / 11→13 intrinsic), `jigc workflow <W> --task <id>` re-entry (W-equality guard + provision-on-first-entry), the write-time `--task` barrier + copy-on-first-touch (`edited-from-base` reachable from the binary), the adapter `spawn:` binding + decidable install-time template validation, the `sub-task` + `milestone-execution` pack workflows entered via `jigc milestone execute`, and per-sub-task authored commits gated by `finalize.fan-out.squash` (default `true`).

**[milestone-completion audit](implementation/milestone-completion-workflow.md): e2e overall-pass + code-review "deliverable genuinely holds"; 3 findings + 1 caveat, all human-triaged + remediated + re-verified.**
- (FIXED `bb6a09c`) **MEDIUM** `squash:false` finalize was non-transactional — N per-sub-task commits landed before the aggregate with no rollback → captures the pre-finalize HEAD and `git reset --hard`s to it on aggregate failure (all-or-nothing); `squash:true` default byte-identical.
- (FIXED `7e5393a`) **MEDIUM** the finalize-less `sub-task` workflow leaked into the router's selectable catalog — and the relaxation had been papered across the routing test **+ three production goldens** (`e2e_audit`/`start_compose`/`pack_source_determinism`) → a `selectable: false` workflow front-matter flag (default true) excludes it + exempts it from the when-required check; all four assertions tightened to assert exclusion. (Scope-honesty: the audit caught a leak the builder had hidden by widening the goldens.)
- (FIXED `c5e7972`) **LOW** sub-task working areas weren't cleaned on a landed milestone finalize → removed on the success path only (rollback path untouched, so F1's reset-on-failure retry still works).
- (BUILT `73dd243`) **caveat** the install-time template rejection path was binary-unreachable → the `JIGC_ADAPTERS_DIR` seam (above) makes it binary-observable.

**The shippability gate — flow-10 Half B (the genuine concurrent Task-tool spawn) — was driven by the orchestrator (main session), the named owner, since a headless build/Workflow subagent cannot spawn the Task tool.** Procedure ([worked-examples.md](design/worked-examples.md) flow 10 Half B): stood up the identical Half-A T3 fixture, ran `jigc milestone execute` to emit the 3 id-sorted `Spawn:` directives, launched **3 genuinely concurrent sub-agents** from those directives (each re-entering via `jigc workflow sub-task --task <id>`, writing only through the `--task`-scoped CLI, authoring the fixed fixture content — prose held fixed because the determinism boundary makes only *structure* comparable), then re-derived state from the CLI → join → `jigc milestone finalize`. **Result: the committed tree-hash (`18d6063…`) is byte-identical to Half A's golden** (the N-process sim), the collision-suffix landed by task id (`add-a-cache-strategy-adr`→bare `cache-strategy`, `document-the-cache-strategy`→`cache-strategy-2`), the synthesized message is the id-ordered projection, and the **blackboard invariant is witnessed** (all three sub-agent transcripts: only `jigc` calls, no git, no sibling-area access). This is the non-hollow proof — a real concurrent launch reaching the same CLI payload + join the sim exercises, not a sequential-in-process simulation. M8 is **shippable**.

**Harness:** this cycle hardened the gates with the **M8 faces** of validation principles #4 (the adapter-rendered launch line is an emitted-bytes contract — render + execute it), #5 (a marker that parses but leaves compose output unchanged is parsed-but-ignored), #7 (drive a write through the barrier from the binary, attempt out-of-bounds), plus the **spawn-class completion artifact** (the orchestrator-owned genuine spawn) in [milestone-completion-workflow.md](implementation/milestone-completion-workflow.md) + the e2e-tester/validator agent defs. No new numbered principle was needed — the M7 "widen + name the new face" lesson held. Next on the spine: **M9 · project setup (new & existing)**.

## 2026-06-05 — M8 retro: four learnings folded back

The M8 run was clean *because the harness mostly worked* — the forward review pre-caught the spawn-owner gap, the planner halted on the inc-5 fork instead of guessing, the audit caught a masking leak the per-increment gates passed. Four learnings folded back (widening disciplines, not adding numbered principles):

- **An acceptance-flow's *invocation commands* are CLI-surface claims, not just its asserted outputs → [milestone-planning](implementation/milestone-planning-workflow.md) Settle.** The inc-5 halt traced to a planning miss: flow-10 wrote `jigc start --workflow … --task …` against the `--workflow conflicts_with --task` exclusivity reaffirmed in the *same* Settle. The "spike every acceptance flow" discipline existed but was applied to engine behaviour, not the invocation grammar. Widened: spike the flow's `$ jigc …` commands against the built command grammar (run them / read `conflicts_with`) — moves the catch from a build halt to a Settle fix.
- **A golden widened to *admit* output is a masking test at the snapshot layer → [increment-workflow.md](implementation/increment-workflow.md) #4 + the validator/code-reviewer agents.** F2's catalog leak was papered across the routing test **+ three production goldens**, all green; the per-increment validators passed it, only the audit caught it. Named: an output-*adding* snapshot diff is a scope claim to adjudicate, and the *same* addition across N goldens is a propagated leak.
- **The determinism boundary applies to the harness itself → [ideas/self-hosting.md](ideas/self-hosting.md).** M8's genuine-spawn proof is un-automatable *by the boundary the product draws* (a headless subagent can't spawn the Task tool). The fix was an **owner-assigned recorded artifact + the automated gate declaring it didn't run**, not a probe — a **third** mechanizable-vs-judgment sort-outcome (step · judgment · owner-assigned artifact) the self-hosting pack must model.
- **"Interrupted" ≠ "halted" on resume → the [milestone-build](.claude/workflows/milestone-build.js) script resume header.** An interruption (process killed mid-call → incomplete call, natural cache-miss, often a dirty tree) resumes by reverting the partial work + plain-resume; a halt (clean tree, cached halt-result) needs the RESUME-note cache-bust surgery. M8 hit both; the distinction is now documented up front.

## 2026-06-06 — M9 (project setup — new & existing) planning: scope settled

The last spine milestone, planned via [`/milestone-plan`](.claude/commands/milestone-plan.md) (scope → detect gaps → settle → review → decompose). M9's risk is **scope, not a novel failure class** (it's setup/ingestion, not a new concurrency/determinism family) — so the win condition was **scope discipline at Settle**: bound the research-grade legacy-ingestion to a tractable, acceptance-testable slice, or defer it. Four `capability-auditor` subagents established a spiked baseline; four `gap-detector`s + (next) an independent `design-reviewer` shaped the design. Home of record for the design: the **new [project-setup.md](design/project-setup.md)** (reading order: after `assistant-adapter`, before `worked-examples`); acceptance = [worked-examples.md](design/worked-examples.md) → **flows 11 (new) + 12 (existing)**.

**The framing call — command vs workflow.** `jigc setup` (the *install command*: adapter + allowlist + `.jigc/config/` init + spawn-template validation) is **built and proven** (M1 inc-6 + M8); M9 adds the *agent-followed setup workflows* on top. M9 does **not** re-touch the install machinery. The baseline audit verified the install is idempotent + non-destructive (structure-aware host-file merge preserves a pre-existing `CLAUDE.md`/`settings.json`), but only *unit*-tested — so M9 lifts no-clobber/idempotency to a **binary-level acceptance** (its cheapest irreversibility proof).

**G1 — existing-project ingestion bounded to detect-and-route; auto-migrate deferred.** The honest line the spike surfaced: a **detect-and-route** classifier (repo-wide discovery → classify each candidate against the persisted schemas via the existing `parse::parse_sections` + `validate::schema_conformance` → binary verdict `adoptable` / `needs-reconcile`-routed / `unmanaged`-ignored) is **net-new but bounded** engine work. **Auto-migrate** (fuzzy-mapping a non-conformant doc into shape) is the research-grade core the strict canonical parser deliberately can't do → **deferred with trigger** ([decisions-pending.md](implementation/decisions-pending.md) → post-spine). Critical spiked correction to the handover's "reuse reconciliation" intuition: the OOB machinery solves the **inverse** problem (drift of jigc-*minted* docs), and its `UNKNOWN → baseline-adopt` path **silently adopts any `.md` under a `location:` dir without a schema check** (a freeform `notes.md` was adopted as an ADR). M9 **closes that safety hole** — adopt only what classifies conformant; never silently absorb. ⇒ chose **bounded detect-and-route** over defer-whole or report-only.

**G2 — `prd` driven by M9, fixed prose slots, zero engine.** `prd` earns its schema from its real driver (the idea-development flow), per the "don't pre-define" discipline. Requirements are **fixed prose slots** (`vision`/`requirements`/`context`, single-word ids) → **pure pack data, zero engine** (baseline-proven: a brand-new `prd` authors create→finalize→promote-to-`prds/` against the unmodified binary). The alternative — repeatable `requirements` — would hit the **stubbed `add-item` authoring wall** (no CLI verb; engine fns test-only) and a **newly-found latent defect** (multi-word section ids make the whole doc non-reparseable — writer title-cases `out-of-scope`→"Out Of Scope", parser flat-compares the raw id). Both **deferred with triggers**; `prd`'s single-word prose slots route around both. ⇒ chose **fixed-slot prd**; the `prd —decomposes-into→ spec` edge stays intent (no per-requirement anchors to author).

**G3 — own `project-setup` workflow, `creates-task: true`.** A new pure-pack-data workflow mirroring the proven `plan` shape (mint → author the `prd` via the create-gate → code-less `docs(prd)` finalize). The idea-development altitude (a `prd` above specs) is distinct enough from `plan` (which assumes you know the change) to earn its own workflow. ⇒ over reuse-`plan` / `creates-task: false` orient-route.

**D1 — "set up" stays "the command ran."** The predicate remains `.jigc/config/` present; the setup workflows are ordinary catalog workflows surfaced in the *clean* state (spiked: a setup workflow composes fine on an already-"clean" project — nothing assumes "set up" is terminal). The over-promising unset-project copy ("the setup workflow walks the pack choice…" — an unbuilt workflow; "pack choice" vacuous at one pack) is **corrected** in [bootstrap.md](design/bootstrap.md) to route to the install then name the real workflows. No third orientation state.

**Confirmed deferred (not re-flagged):** profiles-beyond-Claude-Code (M9 is Claude-Code-only); multi-pack/pack-choice; spawn/fan-out/override/severity untouched (scope honesty — both flows are ordinary compose + create/author + finalize, or scan + classify + route). The `(D)` legacy-ingestion + profiles items **graduated** out of [decisions-pending.md](implementation/decisions-pending.md) → Before planning M9 (the spine's last milestone-keyed deferrals, now empty).

**Forward-looking process review (the conditional off-substrate phase):** assessed — M9 is new *surface* but not a new *failure class*; its real novelty is **irreversible side-effects on a user's real repo** (corrupting an existing `CLAUDE.md`, an idempotency violation on re-run, an ingest that silently drops/adopts docs). Rather than a separate review phase, those exact risks are **bound into the acceptance obligations**: the binary-level no-clobber + idempotency test, and the `baseline-adopt`-hole closure (adopt nothing un-schema-checked) + the detect-and-route verdict assertions (flow 12). The design-reviewer checks whether those bars actually cover the risks.

Minor cleanups folded in: doctype-map `prd` row M8→M9 drift; the unbuilt `jigc adapter install`/`regenerate` verb-surface honest-status note ([assistant-adapter.md](design/assistant-adapter.md)). Advisory-not-fixed (logged): `create.unknown-doctype` routes to a non-existent `jigc doc types`; `when`-hint non-overlap is unenforced.

**Independent design-review (pre-decompose, spiked):** 2 blocking + 4 advisory, all accepted and **baked back before decompose**. The blockers were both scoping holes in the one net-new piece — the ingest **adopt action**: (B1) flow 12 asserted an adopted doc is "registered into the edge index," but reconciliation's `baseline-adopt` does *neither* a schema-check *nor* an index population → "adopt" is now defined as **net-new** (parse → conformance-gate → `index.absorb_doc` → record hash), explicitly distinct from baseline-adopt; (B2) "adopt" was under-defined on file-mutation + the conformant-but-wrong-location case (a silent-drop risk) → **`adoptable` now requires conformant AND at the schema's `location:`**; adopt is **register-only, never moves/rewrites**; a misplaced-but-conformant doc routes to a human as `needs-reconcile`. Advisories folded: the `needs-reconcile`/`unmanaged` boundary made location-decidable; the binary no-clobber + idempotency test concretely specified in flow 12; `jigc ingest` flagged illustrative/net-new + discovery enumeration sorted (deterministic); the `as: brief` create-gate binding noted as unread in M9. The reviewer verified-as-sound: the multi-word-heading defect characterization, the baseline-adopt hole, the pure-pack-data reuse claims (`project-setup`≈`plan`, `ingest-existing`≈`router` shell), flow-11's built verbs, cross-doc consistency, and the determinism boundary.

**Decomposition** ([roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md) → Milestone 9): **3 risk-tiered increments** — (1) `prd` + the new-project `project-setup` flow + the binary no-clobber/idempotency irreversibility proof (proven substrate, shipped first, seeds the shared `prd` schema); (2) the ingestion engine spine in isolation (repo-wide sorted discovery + the location-aware classifier + the net-new schema-gated adopt action, on fixtures — the #1 net-new risk); (3) the existing-project flow (`jigc ingest` command + `ingest-existing` workflow + flow-12 acceptance) wrapping inc 2. Deliberately deviates from strict milestone-level risk-first (new-project's proven path first) because the gap-spike already retired the ingestion *unknown* and inc 1 seeds the `prd` foundation + de-risks the framing; the ingestion-engine risk is still retired in isolation (inc 2) before its flow (inc 3).

## 2026-06-06 — M9 Increment 1 planning: decomposition

Cut [roadmap.md](implementation/roadmap.md) → M9 Increment 1 ("`prd` + the new-project bootstrap flow — the proven on-ramp + the irreversibility proof") into five ordered, single-concern tasks, each with an observable red step. This increment is the `project-setup`/`prd` analogue of the proven M3-inc-3 `plan`/`spec` flow (DECISIONS line ~1302), so its cut mirrors that one. *Grounding (read the code, exercised against the M9 new shape):* (a) **`prd` is pure pack data, zero engine/CLI source change** — verified, not assumed: `cli/src/doc.rs::schemas()` enumerates **all** `schemas/*.yaml` generically via `pack.list(PackResourceKind::Schemas)` + `load_schema`, and `cli/src/pack.rs` embeds the whole `pack/` tree with `include_dir!` at compile time, so a new `schemas/prd.yaml` is auto-discovered with no registration; `engine::state::create_gated` is **fully generic** over the `allows-create` gate (`AllowsCreate { doc_type, as_role }`) — it admits any gated `type`, mints via the schema set, and binds to the entry's `as:` role — so `{type: prd, as: brief}` reuses it unchanged (this generic-gate path is the one M3-inc-3 found *non*-generic — the gate then read `single-task` hardcoded; that defect was **fixed** so the gate now resolves the task's bound workflow via `state::read_workflow_id`, which is exactly what `project-setup` relies on); promotion is generic over `location:`, so a created `prd` promotes to `prds/` like `spec` → `specs/`; `docs` is already a `commit.type` enum member. (b) The **D1 over-promise lives in `cli/src/render.rs::orientation_unset()`** (the "No domain pack is installed" + "The setup workflow walks the pack choice…" copy, ~lines 393–399) with an inline-snapshot test (`render_orientation_unset_routes_to_setup`, ~lines 474–490) — both must change to the corrected [bootstrap.md](design/bootstrap.md) state-1 copy (route to `jigc setup`, then name the real `project-setup` / `ingest-existing` workflows; "No project config layer is present; the cascade has only pack defaults"). (c) The **binary no-clobber/idempotency proof is net-new**: `tests/setup.rs` asserts idempotency only on a **fresh** repo (no pre-existing host content); flow-12 assertion 3 requires *seeding* a non-trivial `CLAUDE.md` + a `.claude/settings.json` hook, running `jigc setup` twice, and asserting the human content survives **verbatim** + the second run is a **byte-identical no-op**. (d) `tests/finalize_spec_only.rs` is the near-exact template for the flow-11 e2e (swap `spec`→`prd`, `plan`→`project-setup`, `specs/`→`prds/`, two slots→three). *Three claims the executor must PROVE in a red step, not trust (per increment-workflow.md "reuse is a claim"):* (i) `prd`'s **single-word section ids** (`vision`/`requirements`/`context`) round-trip the writer↔parser cleanly — a multi-word id trips the logged title-case reparse defect (G2), so T1 asserts a canonical-write→parse round-trip over the real `prd` schema; (ii) `project-setup` **auto-appears in the selectable router catalog** — `start::selectable_workflows` requires `creates_task && selectable && non-empty when`, so T2 asserts the composed router lists `project-setup` with a `when` hint that does **not** overlap the other selectables (`single-task`/`quick-fix`/`plan`/`implement-from-spec`; `when`-non-overlap is unenforced by code per the M9 advisory, so the test enforces it); (iii) the full create→three-set-slot→code-less-finalize arc lands one `docs(prd)` commit promoting `prds/<slug>.md` (T5, through the real binary). Risk-first, linear: the `prd` schema (T1) before the workflow that authors through its create-gate (T2); the two independent small bullets (D1 copy fix T3, binary idempotency T4) next; the headline e2e (T5) last. *Why this cut:* the smallest set delivering all five *Grouped scope* bullets — the `prd` schema, the `project-setup` workflow+steps, the D1 copy fix, the binary irreversibility proof, and the flow-11 acceptance — no creep into inc 2/3's ingestion engine.

- **T1 (pack — `prd` schema):** ship `schemas/prd.yaml` and prove it round-trips. *Done:* `crates/cli/pack/schemas/prd.yaml` (`type: prd`, `location: prds/`, `id-from: title`, three **single-word** prose-slot sections `vision`/`requirements`/`context`, **no** repeatable section, **no** ref edge — per [project-setup.md](design/project-setup.md) → Flow 1); an engine/CLI test (golden/`schema`-load style, e.g. `doc.rs`'s schema tests) asserts `load_schema(prd.yaml)` succeeds and a hand-authored `prd` instance with all three slots filled **canonical-writes then re-parses idempotently** (the single-word-id round-trip — the red step proving G2's constraint holds for the new shape, never trusting it) — gate green.
- **T2 (pack — `project-setup` workflow + steps + `create-prd` ref):** ship the workflow that authors a `prd` and prove it composes + catalogs. *Done:* `crates/cli/pack/workflows/project-setup.yaml` (`creates-task: true`, `allows-create: [{type: prd, as: brief}]`, a non-overlapping `when:` hint, body = the three step includes); the steps `steps/develop-idea.yaml` (pure-prose reasoning over `{{task.intent}}` — vision/requirements/context), `steps/author-prd.yaml` (emits `{{cli.create-prd}}` then `<<author: {{task.brief#vision}}>>` / `#requirements` / `#context`), `steps/project-finalize.yaml` (emits `{{cli.finalize-task}}`); `config/commands.yaml` gains a `create-prd` entry (`jigc doc create prd --title {agent: title}`, mirroring `create-spec`). Tests: a `start_compose`-style test asserts `jigc start --workflow project-setup "<idea>"` mints a task on `workflow: project-setup`, the composed four-class emit carries the `Run: jigc doc create prd` line (the create-gate) and the three `author:` slot lines, and `{{task.intent}}` interpolates; a `routing_loop`/`start_orientation`-style test asserts bare `jigc start "<intent>"` composes the router whose catalog **lists `project-setup` with its `when`** (auto, `creates-task: true`) and that its `when` shares no salient phrase with the other selectables — gate green.
- **T3 (cli — D1 orientation copy fix):** correct the unset-project over-promise. *Done:* `crates/cli/src/render.rs::orientation_unset()` is rewritten to the [bootstrap.md](design/bootstrap.md) state-1 copy — "This project isn't set up. No project config layer is present; the cascade has only pack defaults." + `Run: \`jigc setup\`` + a sentence that `jigc setup` installs jigc then names the real `project-setup` / `ingest-existing` workflows (no "walks the pack choice"); the `render_orientation_unset_routes_to_setup` inline snapshot is updated to match, and asserts the new copy names both real workflows and contains neither "domain pack is installed" nor "walks the pack choice" — gate green.
